Method: 1) 直接对稠密几何高斯表示进行三角剖分以提取显式网格；2) 提出直接网格化算法高效提取和更新网格；3) 基于平面的拉拽约束动态对齐3D高斯原语到近似局部表面；4) 动态冻结完全优化的历史区域以降低长序列处理中的内存和计算开销。

Result: 在公开数据集上，该方法在渲染质量和重建精度上均优于传统基于高斯的方法。

Conclusion: 该在线框架有效解决了高斯泼溅方法在增量式显式网格重建中的局限性，在保持高质量渲染的同时实现了高效的在线表面重建，显著降低了资源消耗。

Abstract: Incremental scene reconstruction is essential for real-world applications. Although 3D Gaussian Splatting shows strong potential, most existing approaches require offline conversion of the optimized Gaussians into an intermediate implicit field for explicit mesh extraction, which hinders seamless integration with downstream tasks. To address this limitation, we propose a novel online framework that incrementally reconstructs and updates high-fidelity explicit meshes by directly triangulating a dense geometric Gaussian representation, which supports both high-quality rendering and incremental surface reconstruction. Moreover, we present a direct meshing algorithm that efficiently extracts and updates the mesh from the Gaussian set. To ensure mesh accuracy, we enforce a plane-based pulling constraint that dynamically aligns 3D Gaussian primitives to the approximated local surface. Furthermore, our framework significantly reduces memory and computational overhead during long-sequence processing by dynamically freezing fully optimized historical regions. Experiments on public datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms conventional Gaussian-based methods on both rendering quality and reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [Effective Synthetic Image Detection via Noise Residual Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10695)
*Caihui Yan,Gang Cao,Huawei Tian,Zhen Li,Yuhang Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的合成图像检测方法NoiseCluSID，利用预训练Noiseprint++提取噪声残差指纹，通过冻结的Vision Transformer提取多尺度特征并进行自适应加权融合，仅需少量真实图像样本初始化K-Means聚类中心，即可区分真实与合成图像。在四个基准数据集上平均准确率达82.2%，泛化能力优于现有方法，尤其对扩散模型生成的图像效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成图像检测方法依赖有监督训练和大规模标注数据，训练成本高且对未知生成模型的泛化性能差。为降低训练成本并提升跨生成模型的泛化能力，本文提出一种无需训练的检测方法。

Method: 1) 使用预训练Noiseprint++模型提取噪声残差指纹；2) 利用冻结的Vision Transformer从残差中提取多尺度特征并进行自适应加权融合；3) 仅需少量真实图像样本初始化K-Means聚类中心，以无监督方式区分真实与合成图像，无需训练过程。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上平均准确率达82.2%，优于现有最先进的检测器；对扩散模型生成的合成图像表现尤为出色；消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效、低成本且泛化能力强的无训练合成图像检测方案，通过噪声残差与无监督聚类相结合的方式，有效解决了传统监督方法的局限性，尤其适用于未知生成模型的检测场景。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative artificial intelligence (AI) has made synthetic images remarkably realistic, posing security threats such as misinformation and fraud. It is significant to detect the synthetic image in the manner of passive and blind image authentication. Most existing detectors rely on supervised training with large labeled datasets, leading to high costs and degraded performance on unknown generative models. To attenuate such deficiencies, we propose a training-free detection method. Specifically, noise residual fingerprints are first extracted by a simple yet effective pre-trained Noiseprint++ model. Then multi-scale features are further extracted from such residual by a frozen Vision Transformer (ViT), followed by adaptive weighted fusion. Only a few real image samples are used needed to initialize the clustering centers for unsupervised K-Means, distinguishing real and synthetic images without training. Extensive evaluations on four benchmark datasets show that our proposed scheme achieves an average accuracy of 82.2%, outperforming the state-of-the-art detectors on generalization ability. Superior performance is gained on the popular diffusion type of synthetic images, and the effectiveness of each module is validated by ablation studies. Source code will be publicly available at https://github.com/multimediaFor/NoiseCluSID.

</details>


### [86] [Traj-VLN: Learning Pixel-Space Interaction via Autoregressive Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10744)
*Changfei Fu,Guangcheng Chen,Wenjun Xu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种在2D像素空间通过自回归轨迹生成来微调视觉语言模型的方法，以解决连续环境中的视觉语言导航任务，在有限计算资源和训练数据下达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN任务可分解为一系列3D空间交互的子任务，但VLMs主要基于RGB图像训练，难以处理涉及深度感知的导航交互，传统引入深度或3D几何信息的方法与VLMs预训练数据不匹配。

Method: 不引入深度/3D信息，而是直接在2D像素空间微调VLMs：给定语言指令和历史观测，模型通过自回归方式依次预测一系列像素坐标，在当前观测的底部中心绘制轨迹。

Result: 像素空间轨迹监督显著提升VLN性能；旗舰模型在有限计算资源和训练数据下达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: 像素空间的轨迹监督优于离散动作学习和像素目标监督，为VLN提供了一种高效且资源友好的新范式。

Abstract: Benefiting from the powerful priors embedded in large-scale pre-training data and the emerging commonsense reasoning ability, large language models (LLMs) have shown unprecedented generalization capabilities in many research fields. Recently, projecting visual embeddings into the language space via vision-language models (VLMs) to achieve sim-toreal and cross-scene generalization has become a prevailing paradigm in the field of Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE). VLN requires an embodied agent to navigate through unseen environments following natural linguistic instructions. We emphasize that a VLN task can be decomposed into a sequence of sub-tasks, each corresponding to a process of 3D spatial interaction with the environments described by instructions such as "walk to the end of the sofa and turn left." However, such spatial interactions involving moving into the image along the direction of depth sensing are puzzling for VLMs as they were predominantly trained on conversations with RGB images. Rather than incorporating depth or 3D geometric information-which VLMs rarely encounter during pretrainingwe propose an alternative approach: fine-tuning VLMs to learn navigation interactions directly in 2D pixel space through autoregressive trajectory generation. Given a linguistic instruction and historical observations, our model sequentially predicts a series of pixel coordinates, drawing a trajectory from the bottom center of the current observation. While prior work has proved that pixel-goal supervision outperforms learning of discrete actions, our experiments further verify that the supervision of pixel-space trajectory significantly enhances VLN performance. Moreover, we demonstrate that our flagship model achieves state-of-the-art level performance with relatively limited computational resources and training data.

</details>


### [87] [Water Reflection Detection Using Symmetric Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10749)
*Shuxuan Yao,Chengjia Wang,Jianyuan Sun,Junyu Dong,Xinghui Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对水面反射导致的目标检测和语义分割性能下降问题，本文利用水的非完美反射对称性，提出SAWRD-Net，它结合二面体群等变卷积和矩阵分解解码器，在最大公开数据集上达到0.890的真阳性率，优于现有所有水反射检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水面反射会导致深度学习模型将物体与其镜像混淆，产生大量假阳性和假阴性，严重影响目标检测和场景理解。因此，准确检测自然水面场景中的反射轴至关重要。

Method: 提出SAWRD-Net，核心包括：(1) 二面体群等变卷积层提取编码旋转和镜像对称性的几何一致特征；(2) 多尺度反射等变块通过对称注意力机制聚合特征并突出反射相关区域；(3) 矩阵分解解码器将高维特征分解为低秩参数和置信空间，直接回归反射轴关键点；(4) 鲁棒主成分分析拟合最终的反射轴。

Result: 在最大公开水反射场景数据集上，SAWRD-Net的真阳性率达到0.890， outperforms 所有现有水反射检测方法。

Conclusion: 利用非完美反射对称性结合群等变卷积与矩阵分解解码器，有效提升了水反射检测的准确性，为水面场景下的可靠目标检测奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reflections of water pose a significant challenge for computer vision systems, as standard deep learning models frequently confuse objects with their mirror images, producing spurious false positives and negatives in tasks such as object detection and semantic segmentation. As a result, detecting reflection axes in natural-water scenes is pivotal for reliable object detection and scene understanding. To mitigate this issue, we leverage the intrinsic imperfect reflective symmetry of water and introduce a Symmetry-Aware Water Reflection Detection Network, namely, SAWRD-Net, that couples dihedral group-equivariant convolutions with a matrix-decomposition decoder in an end-to-end framework. First, dihedral group convolutional layers extract geometry-consistent feature maps that explicitly encode both rotational and mirror symmetries. A Multi-scale Reflection Equivariant block then aggregates features across scales and employs a symmetric-attention mechanism to highlight reflection-relevant regions. The proposed matrix-decomposition decoder factorizes high-dimensional features into compact low-rank parameter and confidence spaces, after which the network directly regresses keypoints on the reflection axis. Then a robust principal component analysis fits the final axis. Evaluated on the largest available water reflection scene data set, SAWRD-Net achieves a true-positive rate of 0.890 against human annotations, outperforming all existing water reflection detectors.

</details>


### [88] [TriCons-Pose: Triangle-Invariant Geometric Consistency Learning for Category-Level Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10754)
*Zuzhi Yang,Shuai Wang,Mounir Kaaniche,Ziwei Li,Zhiming Cheng,Zhidong Zhao,Chenggang Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TriCons-Pose，通过三角不变几何一致性学习解决类别级物体姿态估计中对应点几何不稳定的问题，在REAL275、CAMERA25和HouseCat6D数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于关键点对应范式的类别级姿态估计方法过度依赖特征学习，忽视了所建立对应点在不同扰动下的几何稳定性，导致在类内形状变化和遮挡情况下姿态恢复脆弱。

Method: 提出TriCons-Pose框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 结构一致关键点检测器(SCKD)，通过归一化成对距离匹配强制跨视角结构一致性来识别鲁棒关键点；2) 姿态不变几何聚合器(PIGA)，将基于三角形的姿态不变描述子注入局部到全局的注意力机制中增强关键点表示。优化时除标准目标函数外还加入了额外的几何一致性损失。

Result: 在REAL275、CAMERA25和HouseCat6D三个数据集上的大量实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入三角不变几何一致性学习，TriCons-Pose能锚定稳定关键点并聚合姿态不变线索，产生可靠的规范映射和准确的姿态估计，有效解决了现有方法在形状变化和遮挡下的鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Category-level object pose estimation is a crucial yet challenging task in both academia and industry, and has achieved remarkable success by leveraging keypoint-based correspondence paradigms. However, most existing methods increasingly rely on stronger feature learning while overlooking whether the established correspondences are geometrically stable across diverse perturbations. This often results in fragile pose recovery under intra-class shape variations and occlusions. To tackle this challenge, we develop a novel Triangle-Invariant Geometric Consistency Learning for Category-Level Object Pose Estimation (TriCons-Pose) to anchor stable keypoints and aggregate pose-invariant cues, yielding reliable canonical mapping and accurate pose estimation. Specifically, a Structure-Consistent Keypoint Detector (SCKD) is designed to identify robust keypoints by enforcing cross-view structural consistency via normalized pairwise distance matching. Moreover, we propose a Pose-Invariant Geometric Aggregator (PIGA) to augment keypoint representations by injecting triangle-based pose-invariant descriptors into a local-to-global attention mechanism. The proposed framework is optimized using standard objective functions while incorporating an additional geometry consistency loss. Extensive experiments on REAL275, CAMERA25, and HouseCat6D datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [89] [TOLiD: Bridging the Architecture Gap in Vision Foundation Model to LiDAR Pretraining via Token Lifting for Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10762)
*Sutharsan Mahendran,Darshana Priyasad,Kaushik Roy,Tharindu Fernando,Sridha Sridharan,Clinton Fookes,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TOLiD通过将LiDAR骨干网络与视觉Transformer蒸馏对齐，解决了跨模态和跨架构差距问题，在多个LiDAR数据集上提升了表征迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从视觉基础模型（VFM）到LiDAR骨干的跨模态蒸馏方法将VFM视为冻结特征源，强制学生网络同时弥合模态差距（图像vs点云）和跨架构差距（密集ViT vs稀疏3D编码器），导致特征对齐困难。

Method: TOLiD将LiDAR骨干与一个从冻结VFM教师初始化的学生ViT耦合，通过Frustum Pooling和Frustum Attention将每个图像patch视锥内的点特征转换为token，并采用可见性掩蔽进行token级蒸馏；推理时通过掩蔽双线性采样将token特征回传到逐点表示。

Result: 在5个异质LiDAR数据集和4个跨传感器适配对上进行评估，展示了冻结骨干和轻量级头部的改进迁移性能。

Conclusion: TOLiD通过token级蒸馏策略有效解决了跨模态和跨架构的特征对齐问题，为LiDAR自监督预训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cross-modal distillation from Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) to LiDAR backbones has recently emerged as a self-supervised pretraining strategy that reduces reliance on dense point-wise annotation for 3D scene understanding. However, existing distillation pipelines typically treat the VFM as a frozen feature source and train a heterogeneous 3D backbone to match fixed image embeddings, forcing the student to bridge both the modality gap and the cross-architecture gap between dense ViT token representations and sparse 3D encoders. We propose TOLiD, a self-supervised pretraining method for LiDAR representation learning that addresses this gap by coupling a LiDAR backbone with a student Vision Transformer (ViT) initialized from a frozen VFM teacher and applying supervision over compatible patch-token representations. TOLiD converts the set of point features within each image patch frustum into a token using Frustum Pooling followed by Frustum Attention, and performs token-level distillation with visibility masking. For LiDAR-only deployment, we lift token features back to per-point representations using masked bilinear sampling to avoid patches that have limited LiDAR points. We extensively evaluate TOLiD on five heterogeneous LiDAR datasets and four cross-sensor adaptation pairs, demonstrating improved transfer with frozen backbones and lightweight heads.

</details>


### [90] [RED-Sphere: Hyperspherical Residual Edge Debiasing for Cross-Population Fundus Disease Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10777)
*Yan Lin,Ziheng Wang,Shuang Chen,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei,Stephen McGough*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RED-Sphere，一种即插即用的鲁棒性框架，用于解决医学图像分类器在不可见目标人群上的领域偏移问题。该方法通过边缘与特征能量先验估计捷径敏感干扰、残差软门控衰减主导响应、反事实一致性分离损失正则化、以及归一化球面原型预测，在严格源域-only跨人群设定下，在AMD和DR两个任务上取得一致提升（F1平均增益1.28和2.98），且不依赖任何目标域数据或群体标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类器通常在单一源人群上训练，但临床部署需要面对外观、采集风格、疾病患病率均不同的目标人群。现有公平性与鲁棒性方法要么需要群体标签作为监督，要么将外观变化视为无差别的 nuisance，当人群相关的低级视觉线索与病灶证据共享边缘和纹理结构时，这些方法不足以应对。

Method: RED-Sphere框架包含四个组件：1）利用边缘和特征能量先验估计捷径敏感干扰响应；2）通过残差软门控衰减主导的干扰响应；3）对遮蔽后的干扰视图施加反事实一致性损失和分离损失进行正则化；4）采用归一化球面原型进行标签预测，偏好角度语义证据而非源域相关的激活幅度，同时保留病灶结构信息。

Result: 在严格White-only Harvard-FairVision协议下，RED-Sphere在所有20个任务和骨干网络对比中提升了留出集macro-F1，AMD平均提升1.28 F1点，DR平均提升2.98 F1点。AUC、PR-AUC、可视化诊断、消融实验和敏感性分析进一步支持了更强的外部语义对齐和更稳定的角度疾病几何结构。

Conclusion: RED-Sphere是一种即插即用的鲁棒性框架，不依赖目标域数据或群体监督，在严格源域-only跨人群设置下有效提升医学图像分类器的外部泛化能力。该方法不限于视网膜解剖结构，可通过模态特异的nuisance先验适配到其他外观捷径与语义证据纠缠的场景。

Abstract: Medical image classifiers are often trained within one source population, yet clinical deployment requires robustness to patients whose appearance, acquisition style, and disease prevalence differ from the source cohort. Existing fairness and robustness methods often require group supervision or treat appearance variation as an undifferentiated nuisance, which is insufficient when population-correlated low-level cues and lesion evidence share edge and texture structure. We study a strict source-only cross-population setting, where external populations are unseen during optimization, validation, scheduling, hyperparameter and model selection. We propose RED-Sphere, a plug-and-play robustness framework for image classification under unseen population shifts. It estimates shortcut-sensitive nuisance responses with an edge and feature energy prior, attenuates dominant responses through residual soft gating, regularizes masked nuisance views with counterfactual-inspired consistency and separation losses, and predicts labels with normalized spherical prototypes. It favours angular semantic evidence over source-correlated activation magnitude while preserving lesion structure. Although demonstrated on 2D Scanning Laser Ophthalmoscopy (SLO) fundus classification for Age-Related Macular Degeneration (AMD) and Diabetic Retinopathy (DR), RED-Sphere is not tied to retinal anatomy: the same principle can be adapted with modality-specific nuisance priors wherever appearance shortcuts and semantic evidence are entangled. Under a strict White-only Harvard-FairVision protocol, RED-Sphere improves held-out macro-F1 across all 20 task and backbone comparisons, with average gains of 1.28 and 2.98 F1 points on AMD and DR. Gains in AUC and PR-AUC, visual diagnostics, ablations, and sensitivity analyses further support stronger external semantic alignment and more stable angular disease geometry.

</details>


### [91] [Is Energy Guidance All You Need? Training-Free Norm Injection for Driving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10781)
*Xiyan Su,Frank Diermeyer,Markus Lienkamp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究在不重新训练扩散主干网络的情况下，通过在采样时注入可微能量函数来引导整流流驾驶世界模型的规划轨迹，实现符合交通规范的受控视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大规模视频扩散的驾驶世界模型能生成逼真场景，但缺乏可控性——强制执行交通规范通常需要重新训练主干网络或用人工构建的布局进行条件化。作者质疑这种可控性是否必须依赖训练。

Method: 基于整流流（rectified-flow）的驾驶世界模型，联合生成未来视频和规划的自我轨迹；在采样时通过可微能量函数注入引导（energy guidance），无需针对特定知识重新训练扩散主干；采用Open-Sora 2.0 MM-DiT作为主干网络。

Result: 成功展示了通过能量引导使模型在反事实目标位置刹车，但生成的视频尚未能完全跟随被引导的轨迹穿过主干网络的联合自注意力机制。

Conclusion: 跨流耦合（cross-stream coupling）是实现端到端可控展开的关键要求。

Abstract: Driving world models built on large video-diffusion backbones generate realistic scenes but are hard to control: enforcing a traffic norm typically means retraining the backbone or conditioning it on hand-built layouts. We ask whether controllability requires training at all. Our experiment shows that a rectified-flow driving world model, which jointly generates future video and a planned ego trajectory, can have its planned trajectory steered entirely at sampling time by differentiable energy functions that encode driving norms, without knowledge-specific retraining of the diffusion backbone. Concretely, we demonstrate that a world model built on Open-Sora 2.0 MM-DiT backbone can be steered to brake at a counterfactual target by injecting energy guidance at sampling time. However, we find that the generated video does not yet follow the steered trajectory through the backbone's joint self-attention and identify the cross-stream coupling as a crucial requirement for end-to-end-controllable rollouts.

</details>


### [92] [Toward Efficient Weakly Supervised Semantic Segmentation Using Only Low-Magnification Histopathological Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10783)
*Dung Minh Do,Nhat-Thanh Huynh,Duc Minh Huynh,Doanh C. Bui,Khang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在弱监督和低分辨率存储设置下，全切片病理图像的分割性能。通过模拟低倍率输入并重建，发现重建质量指标不能直接预测下游分割性能，并识别出小尺度结构定位显著下降的关键退化点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高倍率全切片图像存储和传输资源消耗大，且像素级标注成本高。因此需要探究低倍率图像配合弱监督（图像级标签）是否能达到与高倍率图像相当的性能，以指导数字病理存储系统的优化设计。

Method: 从高分辨率图像块出发，模拟低倍率输入，然后使用插值和基于深度学习的方法重建为原始尺寸，再应用弱监督分割流水线，定量评估不同分辨率退化程度下弱监督方法的表现。

Result: 重建质量指标不足以预测下游分割性能；存在一个关键退化点，在该点之后小尺度结构的定位性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 研究为设计高效的数字病理存储系统同时保持可靠的自动化分析提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Whole-slide images (WSIs) provide rich tissue-level and cellular-level information, but storing and transmitting high-magnification pathology data is resource-intensive. Moreover, annotating WSIs at the pixel level is labor-intensive and time-consuming. Therefore, it is important to investigate whether low-magnification pathology images with limited annotations (i.e., image-level instead of pixel-level labels) can achieve performance comparable to high-magnification images. This paper presents a systematic benchmark study on weakly supervised histopathological image segmentation under different low-resolution storage settings. Starting from high-resolution image patches, we simulate lower-magnification inputs and reconstruct them to the original size using interpolation and deep learning-based reconstruction methods before applying the weakly-supervised segmentation pipeline. This framework enables a quantitative evaluation of how weakly supervised methods respond to different levels of resolution degradation. Experimental results show that reconstruction quality metrics alone are insufficient to predict downstream segmentation performance. In particular, the study identifies a critical degradation point where the localization of small-scale structures declines significantly. These findings provide practical guidance for designing efficient digital pathology storage systems while maintaining reliable automated analysis. Code is available at https://github.com/Dung-Dx/LowMagWSS

</details>


### [93] [Detecting AI-Generated Video: A Vision-Language Dual-View Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10787)
*Dylan Xinming Hou,Juntian Zhang,Xu Gu,Yichen Wu,Nils Lukas,Gus Xia,Xiuying Chen,Yuhan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将AIGC视频检测重新定义为"事实保真度验证"，并构建了视觉-语言双视角的四层分类体系，系统综述了从低级伪影检测到高级语义验证的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于伪影的检测方法在日益逼真的AI生成视频面前已显不足，需要从低级特征检测转向高级语义验证的新范式。

Method: 提出视觉-语言双视角分类法，将检测方法组织为四层层次结构：内在线索分析、时空一致性建模、跨模态一致性推理、语言引导的世界级推理；系统综述221篇相关工作。

Result: 构建了从伪影匹配到基于证据的语义验证的转变框架，综述了AIGC-V生成范式、检测方法、评估指标与基准数据集。

Conclusion: 讨论了当前挑战，指出了向鲁棒、可解释、可信赖检测方向发展的未来研究机遇。

Abstract: The evolving realism of AI-generated Videos (AIGC-V) is rapidly rendering traditional artifact-centric detection insufficient, necessitating a paradigm shift from low-level inspection to high-level semantic verification. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of AIGC-V detection, reframing the task as Factual Fidelity Verification, which asks whether the events, entities, and physical processes depicted in a video are consistent with real-world facts. To systematize this rapidly evolving field, we propose a Vision-Language Dual-View taxonomy that organizes existing methods into a hierarchical, four-layer landscape, spanning intrinsic cue analysis, spatiotemporal consistency modeling, cross-modal consistency reasoning, and language-guided world-level reasoning. This dual-view framing highlights a fundamental transition from artifact matching in traditional deepfake detection to evidence-based semantic verification enabled by vision-language models and agentic reasoning pipelines. Based on a systematic review of 221 works, we synthesize AIGC-V generation paradigms, survey the landscape of detection methods, and review evaluation metrics and benchmarks in line with proposed views. Finally, we discuss current challenges and identify promising directions toward robust, explainable, and trustworthy detection.

</details>


### [94] [MAC-Splat: Multi-Attribute Consistency for High-Fidelity Sparse-View Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10792)
*Jinqian Yang,Yichen Wu,Wanhua Li,Haokun Lin,Renzhen Wang,Xiangchu Feng,Xixi Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAC-Splat提出了一种基于3D一致性监督的稀疏视角神经渲染训练框架，通过MASt3R几何骨干和DINOv3编码器获取语义化2D对应点作为几何锚点，定义多属性一致性损失（MAC loss）联合正则化匹配高斯的位姿、形状和外观属性，在ScanNet++上相比Splatt3R提升PSNR超过4.5dB，有效解决了稀疏视角下的几何伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可泛化3D高斯泼溅方法在稀疏视角设置下仅依赖2D光度损失监督，无法解决深度和对应关系的歧义性，导致几何伪影严重。

Method: 构建基于MASt3R几何骨干和冻结DINOv3编码器的框架，获取语义化2D对应点作为3D监督的几何锚点，进而定义多属性一致性损失（MAC loss），联合正则化匹配高斯的位置、形状和外观属性，使其在公共世界坐标系中保持一致，且对异常值鲁棒并尊重协方差矩阵的几何结构。

Result: 在ScanNet++数据集上，MAC-Splat在所有重叠率设置下均优于强基线，平均PSNR相比Splatt3R提升超过4.5dB，LPIPS降低，且在相机位姿间隔增大时仍能维持性能。

Conclusion: 直接的多属性3D一致性目标结合高质量对应点，能有效解决病态的稀疏视角重建问题。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity 3D scenes from sparse-views remains a central problem in generalizable neural rendering. Existing generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods often exhibit geometric artifacts in sparse-view settings, since supervision based solely on 2D photometric losses cannot resolve depth and correspondence ambiguities. To address this issue, we propose MAC-Splat, a training framework built around direct 3D consistency supervision. MAC-Splat builds on the MASt3R geometric backbone and a frozen DINOv3 encoder to obtain semantically informed 2D correspondences, which serve as geometric anchors for 3D supervision. Using these anchors, we define the Multi-Attribute Consistency (MAC) loss. This objective jointly regularizes the 3D attributes of matched Gaussians, including their position, shape, and appearance, by enforcing agreement in a common world coordinate frame. The formulation is robust to outliers and respects the geometry of covariance matrices, which leads to stable training under sparse-view conditions. Experiments on ScanNet++ show that MAC-Splat outperforms strong baselines, with particularly large gains under different overlap regimes. In particular, it improves average PSNR over Splatt3R by more than 4.5 dB, reduces LPIPS, and maintains performance as the camera pose gap increases. These results indicate that a direct, multi-attribute 3D consistency objective, when combined with high-quality correspondences, is effective for addressing the ill-posed sparse-view reconstruction problem.

</details>


### [95] [Mixture of Cognitive Experts in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10796)
*Robert Wijaya,Ngai-Man Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于布卢姆认知分类学的证据驱动多模态推理框架，通过两阶段认知言语化（字面证据摘要+布卢姆言语化）将多编码器专家输出转化为结构化推理轨迹，并利用轻量级推理轨迹模块进行定量分析，以提升LVLM的可信度、可解释性并减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM对不同认知层次的视觉-语言问题采用相同的感知-推理路由，缺乏细粒度的认知适配；同时多专家编码器的集成缺乏可解释性和可验证性，导致幻觉问题难以缓解。

Method: 1）两阶段认知言语化：先通过字面证据摘要将多专家输出分解为原子化证据陈述，再通过布卢姆言语化生成分阶段推理轨迹；2）轻量级推理轨迹模块对推理轨迹进行定量分析，显式化证据使用和推理进程。

Result: 在感知和推理能力上观察到多项改进；推理轨迹模块的定量分析表明不同查询会诱发不同的认知入口层级和证据使用轨迹，支持细粒度分析。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了多模态推理的可解释性和可验证性，为LVLM的认知层级适配提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) require strong reasoning over both visual and textual input. Recent work suggests that cognitive elements, especially diverse representations and metacognition, correlate with better performance. Many of the needed perceptual functions are already provided by specialized domain-specific computer vision models, which act as the perceptual subsystem for detecting objects, localizing them, inferring states, recovering spatial layout, and reading text. The key challenge is to integrate these multi-encoder experts into a trustworthy, interpretable, and coherent representation that improves verifiability and reduces hallucinations. This is difficult because vision-language questions span different cognitive levels, yet most LVLM pipelines apply the same perception-reasoning routing regardless of the demand of each query. We propose an evidence-driven multimodal reasoning framework that utilizes a Bloom-inspired taxonomy as a hierarchical reasoning protocol. The two-stage cognitive verbalization first produces a Literal Evidence Summary by decomposing expert outputs into short, atomic evidence statements. It then performs Bloom Verbalization to turn these evidence items into a staged reasoning trace, and a lightweight Reasoning Trace Module quantitatively analyzes the trace to make evidence usage and reasoning progression explicit. Through this integration, we observed several improvements in perception and reasoning abilities. Moreover, the trace module provides quantitative evidence that different queries induce different cognitive entry levels and evidence-use trajectories that enable fine-grained analysis.

</details>


### [96] [Compositional Context Fine-Tuning Vision-Language Model for Complex Assembly Action Understanding from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10797)
*Hao Zheng,Jinyi Huang,Tiantian Zheng,Xun Xu,Tuka Alhanai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出组合上下文微调（CCFT）和层分区交替训练（LP-AT）方法，将视觉-语言模型适配于装配动作理解，通过将动作分解为语义元素（动词、对象、工具）并使用模板化问答进行微调，在有限数据下实现高效多任务学习，在两个装配视频数据集上超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配动作理解是人机协作装配的关键，但由于动作细微且涉及细粒度的手-物交互，现有方法难以有效识别。视觉-语言模型在该领域应用不足，需要针对有限数据和复杂语义分解进行适配。

Method: 提出了组合上下文微调（CCFT），将装配动作分解为Verb、Object、Tool三个语义元素，通过模板化问答对微调VLM以实现近确定性输出；同时提出层分区交替训练（LP-AT），为不同语义元素分配不同模型层并配备元素特定的低秩适配器，交替更新权重以减少跨任务干扰并支持独立超参数优化。

Result: 在HA-ViD-VQA和IKEA-ASM-VQA数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多项指标上一致优于强动作识别基线，并提供可解释的元素级预测结果。

Conclusion: CCFT和LP-AT有效解决了装配动作理解中的细粒度交互和有限数据挑战，为下游应用提供了可解释、确定性的动作预测支持。

Abstract: Assembly action understanding is a key enabler for effective human-robot collaborative assembly, yet it remains challenging due to subtle motions and fine-grained hand-object interactions. We adapt vision-language models (VLMs) to this challenging domain with Compositional Context Fine-Tuning (CCFT), a method that decomposes assembly actions into semantic elements (Verb, Object, Tool) and fine-tunes VLMs to recognize each action element using templated question-answering pairs. This approach ensures near-deterministic outputs. To enable efficient and effective multi-task learning under limited data, a Layer-Partitioned Alternating Training (LP-AT) method is presented, which assigns distinct model layers to recognize specific action elements through element-specific low-rank adapters. LP-AT alternates weight updates across element-specific adapters, reducing cross-task interference while enabling per-adapter hyperparameter optimization. Furthermore, we create HA-ViD-VQA and IKEA-ASM-VQA datasets from existing assembly video datasets. Extensive experiments on these datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong action recognition baselines while providing interpretable element-level predictions that can support diverse downstream applications.

</details>


### [97] [h-Flow: Flexible Flow-based Image Editing via Doob's h-Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10800)
*Zehui Guo,Zhen Wang,Junwei Shu,Yang Li,Changbo Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出h-Flow，一个无需训练、理论基础的流模型编辑框架，利用Doob's h-Transform将图像编辑重构为多终条件生成问题，通过速度正交分解实现源一致性与目标对齐的可控平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练文本到图像流模型的编辑方法通常需要仔细平衡目标提示对齐与源图像一致性，且依赖反转管线或启发式源到目标轨迹构建，往往受限于架构特定设计或对超参数敏感。

Method: 首先将Doob's h-Transform从SDE模型扩展到确定性RF框架，通过构造具有相同边际分布的等价SDE；设计专门的h-函数实现源一致性和目标对齐，得到闭式重构引导和基于速度的语义编辑信号；引入速度正交分解解耦重构和编辑方向，实现两目标的可控权衡。

Result: 大量实验表明h-Flow在多种场景下实现了有效、鲁棒且灵活的编辑效果。

Conclusion: h-Flow无需训练、具有理论基础，通过速度正交分解实现了源一致性与目标对齐的可控权衡，为流模型编辑提供了通用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Editing images with pre-trained text-to-image flow models typically requires carefully balancing target alignment with the desired prompt and source consistency with the original image. Existing approaches either rely on inversion-based pipelines or heuristic source-to-target trajectory constructions, which often depend on architecture-specific designs or are sensitive to hyperparameters. In this paper, we propose h-Flow, a training-free and theoretically grounded flow-based editing framework. Inspired by Doob's $h$-Transform, we reformulate image editing as conditional generation under multiple terminal events corresponding to source consistency and target alignment. We first extend the classical $h$-Transform from SDE-based models to the deterministic RF framework by constructing an equivalent SDE with identical marginals. Within this formulation, we design dedicated $h$-functions for source consistency and target alignment, yielding closed-form reconstruction guidance and velocity-based semantic editing signals. We further introduce a velocity orthogonal decomposition to decouple reconstruction and editing directions, enabling a controllable trade-off between the two objectives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that h-Flow achieves effective, robust, and flexible editing across diverse scenarios. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [98] [OmniX: Any-view and Any-time 4D Reconstruction via Feed-forward Trajectory Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10840)
*Yanqin Jiang,Tengfei Wang,Zhengwei Wang,Chenjie Cao,Junta Wu,Wenhan Luo,Weiming Hu,Jin Gao,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniX是一个前馈式4D重建框架，通过解耦动态运动建模与静态几何预测，利用紧凑的动态令牌表示三维运动轨迹，并构建了包含8万场景和128万多视角视频的大规模数据集，在密集3D点轨迹预测、3D点跟踪、视频深度估计和相机位姿估计等任务上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式4D重建方法要么逐帧预测静态点云而忽略前景运动，要么估计点云轨迹但局限于小相机运动，无法在大视角变化下聚合时序观测并重建完整的动态场景。

Method: OmniX将动态运动建模与静态几何预测解耦，利用稀疏低秩的3D运动结构，通过紧凑的动态令牌生成所有像素在所有图像上的轨迹场，同时保持全局交互。此外，构建了基于UE5的自动4D数据引擎，生成了包含8万场景和128万多视角视频的大规模数据集。

Result: 在密集3D点轨迹预测和3D点跟踪任务上达到当前最优，在视频深度估计和相机位姿估计上也展现出有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: OmniX通过解耦运动和几何、利用动态令牌的稀疏低秩表示，有效解决了大相机运动下的动态场景4D重建问题。

Abstract: Previous feed-forward 4D reconstruction methods either predict per-frame static point clouds, ignoring foreground motion, or estimate point cloud trajectories while being limited to small camera motions. This restricts their ability to aggregate observations over time and reconstruct complete dynamic scenes under large viewpoint changes. To address this limitation, we propose OmniX, a feed-forward 4D reconstruction framework that predicts dense 3D point trajectories for every pixel from videos with large camera motion. OmniX decouples dynamic motion modeling from static geometry prediction and represents motion using a compact set of dynamic tokens. By leveraging the sparse and low-rank structure of 3D motion, these tokens generate trajectory fields for all pixels across all images while efficiently preserving global interactions. To facilitate training, we further build an automatic UE5-based 4D data engine and introduce a large-scale dataset containing 80K scenes and 1.28M multi-view videos with full geometric annotations. OmniX achieves state-of-the-art performance on dense 3D point trajectory prediction and 3D point tracking, while also demonstrating competitive results on video depth estimation and camera pose estimation.

</details>


### [99] [Align and Segment: Unsupervised Learning for Building Segmentation From Misaligned Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10841)
*Venkanna Babu Guthula,Oswin Krause,Dimitri Gominski,Hui Zhang,Johan Mottelson,Ankit Kariryaa,Nico Lang,Christian Igel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Align and Segment (AnS)方法，在训练语义分割网络时同时学习标签与图像的配准对齐，通过空间变换模块和自监督正则化损失，无需任何真实标签即可处理标签错位问题，在遥感建筑分割任务上取得了高质量的分割结果和精确的对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像中，来自不同源的图像与标签（如OpenStreetMap）之间常存在空间错位，这种错位会严重损害监督学习的分割性能。现有方法缺乏在无真实标签情况下同时进行标签对齐和分割训练的机制，因此需要一种能够从错位标签中学习并同时校正对齐的方法。

Method: 提出AnS方法，基于空间变换模块（Spatial Transformer Module）对错位标签进行仿射变换，将其对齐到图像空间后作为语义分割网络的训练目标；同时引入自监督正则化损失，防止网络学习到错位标签中的错误模式（即捷径学习）；该方法与数据增强互补，特别适用于系统性错位的数据。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据（来自不同城市）上的实验表明，AnS方法能够同时实现高质量的建筑分割和精确的标签-图像对齐，且无需任何真实标签。

Conclusion: 提出的AnS方法有效解决了遥感图像中标签错位问题，能够在无金标准标签的条件下联合学习对齐与分割，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Supervised learning for image segmentation typically requires spatially aligned image and label sets. When images and labels originate from different sources, the pairing may be misaligned, which can significantly deteriorate the performance of the learned models. This is especially common in remote sensing, when aerial or satellite images are co-registered with labels from another source (e.g., OpenStreetMap). In this work, we propose a novel approach for training on misaligned labels, where we simultaneously learn the label alignment. Our align and segment (AnS) approach builds on the spatial transformer module to transform the misaligned labels using an affine transformation to provide a better learning target for a canonical semantic segmentation network. We prevent shortcut learning of misaligned labels in these semantic segmentation networks through a self-supervised regularization loss and show that it is complementary to data augmentation, especially for systematically misaligned training data. A decisive characteristic of our AnS approach is that it learns without requiring any golden labels. We experimentally show on both synthetic and real-world data from different cities that our approach enables high-quality building segmentation and precise label-image alignment at the same time. Code and derived datasets are available at https://github.com/venkanna37/align-and-segment

</details>


### [100] [Learning To Focus: Anatomy-Guided Attention Regularization for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10851)
*Tonmoy Hossain,Atiqur Rahman,Farhana Hossain Swarnali,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Locus框架，利用预训练分割基础模型提取解剖先验，通过自适应注意力正则化引导分类器关注诊断相关解剖区域，在8个医学影像数据集上提升了分类性能和注意力可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类模型应关注诊断相关区域，但标准分类损失缺乏空间监督。解剖分割掩码能提供空间引导，但获取掩码需要大量人工标注和计算开销。因此，希望利用分割基础模型的定位能力，在不训练专用分割模型的前提下，为分类器提供解剖先验。

Method: 提出Locus框架，利用预训练分割基础模型生成解剖形状先验，引入自适应注意力正则化项：不是强制像素级对齐，而是动态平衡前景（解剖区域）和背景区域的注意力权重，当背景注意力占主导时施加惩罚。

Result: 在涵盖皮肤镜、X光、组织病理学和心脏MRI的8个多样化医学影像数据集上验证，Locus在分类性能和注意力解剖学可解释性方面均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 利用预训练分割基础模型作为先验知识的来源，通过轻量级正则化机制，可以高效提升医学图像分类器的诊断相关区域关注能力，无需额外训练分割模型或像素级对齐约束。

Abstract: Medical image classification models are ideally expected to identify diagnostically relevant regions while making predictions, yet standard classification losses rarely provide spatial supervision. Explicit supervision via anatomical shape information, such as segmentation masks of task-relevant anatomy, has been shown to guide the network toward regions relevant to the target prediction. However, obtaining such masks incurs substantial manual annotation effort and computational overhead. With the advent of segmentation foundation models that exhibit strong localization of anatomical structures across diverse imaging modalities, we leverage this capability to extract anatomical shape priors without the burden of training a dedicated segmentation model. In this paper, we propose a new framework, Locus, an anatomical attention regularization framework that leverages pretrained segmentation foundation models to guide a classifier's attention toward diagnostically meaningful anatomical structures across diverse imaging modalities. Instead of enforcing pixel-wise alignment with the foundation-model-derived mask, we introduce a regularization term that adaptively balances attention between anatomical (foreground) and background regions, penalizing the classifier when background attention dominates. We validate Locus on eight diverse medical imaging datasets spanning dermoscopy, X-ray, histopathology, and cardiac MRI, showing consistent gains in classification performance alongside improved anatomically grounded attention.

</details>


### [101] [Diversify Diffusion with Temperature Sampling and Variance-Corrective Time Shifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10853)
*Peizhuo Li,Emre Aksan,Alexandru-Eugen Ichim,Thabo Beeler,Olga Sorkine-Hornung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需重新训练的推理时方法——方差校正时间偏移（Variance-Corrective Time Shifting），通过查询偏移时间步并缩放分数来消除高温采样（$p_0^{(γ)}(x) \propto p_0(x)^γ$）带来的方差膨胀，从而在保持模式重加权的同时提升样本多样性。在DiT、Stable Diffusion和Motion Diffusion模型上验证了该方法在最小化质量和条件保真度损失下的多样性控制效果，并揭示了温度干预时机可实现从粗到细的多样性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型忠实再现训练分布，但也会继承其不平衡性，使得稀有或欠表达模式难以生成。高温采样（$γ<1$）在理论上可以压平主导模式并提升稀有模式权重，但朴素分数缩放（naive score scaling）在正确重加权模式的同时会膨胀每个模式的方差，破坏反向扩散过程并降低样本质量。因此，需要一种训练无关的修正方案来消除方差膨胀，使温度采样成为实用的多样性控制工具。

Method: 提出方差校正时间偏移（Variance-Corrective Time Shifting）：在推理时查询一个偏移后的时间步（shifted timestep），并将所得分数乘以$γ$。该操作在保持模式重加权效果的同时，消除朴素温度采样引入的方差膨胀。该方法是训练无关的（training-free），无需重新训练模型，可直接应用于预训练的扩散模型和流匹配（flow-matching）骨干网络。

Result: 在DiT、Stable Diffusion和Motion Diffusion模型上，该方法在样本质量和条件保真度损失最小的情况下，持续提升了多样性。进一步发现，温度干预的时机可实现粗到细的控制：高噪声阶段干预影响跨模式的组合多样性（compositional diversity），低噪声阶段干预影响固定组合下的局部外观变化（local appearance variation）。

Conclusion: 方差校正时间偏移将简单温度采样转化为实用的多样性控制旋钮，无需重新训练，在多个扩散模型架构上验证了有效性。该方法纠正了朴素分数缩放的关键缺陷，并揭示了温度干预时机对不同层次多样性的控制能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models faithfully reproduce their training distribution, but also inherit its imbalances and leave rare or under-represented modes hard to reach. A natural inference-time remedy is to sample from the high-temperature target $p^{(γ)}_0(x) \propto p_0(x)^γ$ for $0 < γ< 1$, which flattens dominant modes and lifts rare ones. However, naive score scaling while correctly reweighting modes also inflates the per-mode variance, breaking the reverse diffusion process and degrading sample quality. We introduce variance-corrective time shifting, a training-free fix that queries the network at a shifted timestep and scales the resulting score by $γ$, canceling the variance inflation while preserving the mode reweighting. The correction turns simple temperature sampling into a practical diversity knob for pretrained diffusion and flow-matching backbones with no retraining, and we demonstrate consistent gains at minimal cost to sample quality and condition fidelity across DiT, Stable Diffusion and Motion Diffusion models. We further show that the timing of the temperature intervention enables coarse-to-fine control: high-noise stages drive compositional diversity across modes, while low-noise stages drive local appearance variation under a fixed composition.

</details>


### [102] [AU-Guided Synthetic Video Generation for Micro-Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10860)
*Pei-Sze Tan,Sailaja Rajanala,Yee-Fan Tan,Raphael C. -W. Phan,Huey-Fang Ong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EquiME——一个由AU引导的图像到视频生成技术构建的合成微表情数据集，包含75K视频，覆盖5种目标情绪，并附带人口统计元数据和质量评估。跨数据集实验表明，基于EquiME训练的模型在SAMM和CASME II上取得了有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微表情数据集规模小、人口覆盖窄、情绪标签有限，严重制约了微表情识别研究的发展。

Method: 采用AU（动作单元）引导的图像到视频生成技术，从15K张源人脸图像生成75K个视频片段，覆盖5种目标情绪，并自动推断人口统计元数据和进行视频质量测量。

Result: 通过帧对相似度、空间变化、无参考感知质量指标进行评估，并在SAMM和CASME II上进行跨数据集MER实验。基于EquiME训练的模型在跨数据集场景下取得了有竞争力的性能，且在四种不同架构上表现稳定。

Conclusion: EquiME作为合成微表情识别资源是有效的，论文详细介绍了数据集设计、结构化AU条件生成流程和实证评估证据。

Abstract: Micro-expression recognition is limited by the small scale, narrow demographic coverage, and restricted emotion labels of existing datasets. We introduce EquiME, a synthetic micro-expression dataset built from AU-guided image-to-video generation. EquiME contains 75K videos generated from 15K source face images across five target emotions, together with automatically inferred demographic metadata and video-quality measurements. We evaluate EquiME using frame-pair similarity, spatial variation, and no-reference perceptual-quality metrics, together with cross-dataset MER experiments on SAMM and CASME II. Models trained on EquiME achieve competitive cross-dataset performance on SAMM and CASME II and show comparatively low variation across the four evaluated architectures. This paper focuses on the dataset design, the structured AU-conditioning pipeline used for video generation, and the empirical evidence needed to assess EquiME as a synthetic MER resource. Project page: https://kirito-blade.github.io/me-vlm/

</details>


### [103] [X-GuideAR: An Augmented Reality Framework to Mitigate Radiation Exposure during Fluoroscopic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10873)
*Mingxu Liu,Zixuan Liu,Ruchen Cai,Yu-Chen Ku,Suxi Gu,Amit Jain,Alejandro Martin-Gomez,Mehran Armand*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-GuideAR是一个增强现实框架，通过生成合成X射线预览来减少骨科手术中的辐射暴露，在S2AI螺钉置入实验中减少62.3%的X射线次数，并提高螺钉安全性（平均安全直径12.95mm vs 5.9mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统骨科螺钉置入需要频繁进行X射线对齐检查和多视角解剖成像（即"fluoro-hunting"），导致患者和手术团队承受大量辐射暴露，亟需一种减少辐射的同时保证螺钉置入精度的方法。

Method: 提出X-GuideAR框架：1）通过生成合成X射线预览提供无辐射的视图获取和钻孔引导；2）识别所需解剖视图后采集真实X射线，将钻孔轨迹预览叠加到真实X射线上进行精确引导。

Result: 专家脊柱外科医生进行的8条S2AI轨迹初步研究表明：X射线次数减少62.3%；X-GuideAR辅助下平均安全螺钉直径12.95mm，传统工作流为5.9mm，表明骨性包容性和生物力学优势显著提升。

Conclusion: X-GuideAR在减少辐射暴露和优化S2AI螺钉置入流程方面潜力巨大，为更安全高效的手术提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Achieving optimal screw placement for orthopedic surgeries requires frequent alignment checks and multiple anatomical views under X-ray -- a process known as "fluoro-hunting" that increases radiation exposure to patients and surgical teams. This work introduces X-GuideAR, an augmented reality (AR) framework for identifying optimal X-ray views, aimed at reducing radiation exposure while ensuring accurate screw placement. To exemplify the benefits of X-GuideAR, we focus on S2 alar-iliac (S2AI) screw placement. Our system provides radiation-free guidance for view acquisition and drilling by generating synthetic X-ray previews that accelerate fluoro-hunting. Once the required anatomical views are identified using these previews, a real X-ray is acquired, and the preview of the drilling trajectory is augmented onto it, facilitating precise screw placement with minimal additional radiation. A preliminary study involving eight S2AI trajectories performed by an expert spine surgeon demonstrated a 62.3% reduction in the number of X-rays. Post-procedure evaluations showed that trajectories done with X-GuideAR supported an average safe screw diameter of 12.95 mm compared to 5.9 mm under the conventional workflow, suggesting improved bony containment and potential biomechanical benefit. X-GuideAR shows great potential to reduce radiation exposure and streamline S2AI screw placement, offering a promising direction toward safer and more efficient surgeries.

</details>


### [104] [Design Choices in Splitting-Based Self-Supervised Sparse-View CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10898)
*Nadja Gruber,Lukas Neumann,Ander Biguri,Gyeongha Hwang,Markus Haltmeier,Johannes Schwab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了自监督数据分割稀疏CT重建中的三个关键设计要素（划分策略、预处理、推理），通过统一框架对比现有方法并提出两种增量扩展（多分区划分和替代推理策略）。实验表明：最优划分策略高度依赖噪声结构——格基划分在独立噪声下表现好，角掩码在相关噪声和真实数据中更鲁棒；多分区划分在多种设置中优于纯投影划分；感知指标（LPIPS、HaarPSI）揭示了掩码策略间差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督数据分割方法虽已用于稀疏CT重建，但其关键设计选择（划分策略、预处理、推理）的影响尚不明确，缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 提出将重建分解为划分策略、预处理、推理三组件的统一框架，对现有方法进行受控对比，并提出多分区划分和替代推理策略两种增量扩展。

Result: 格基划分在独立噪声下最优，角掩码在相关噪声和真实测量数据中更鲁棒；多分区划分在多个设置中一致优于纯投影划分；LPIPS和HaarPSI等感知指标揭示了PSNR和SSIM难以捕捉的差异。

Conclusion: 为自监督稀疏CT重建方法设计提供了实用指南，并揭示了真实成像环境中常见独立性假设的局限性。

Abstract: Self-supervised data splitting has emerged as a promising paradigm for sparse-view CT reconstruction, enabling training from incomplete measurements without fully sampled ground truth. However, the influence of key design choices, including partitioning strategy, preprocessing, and inference, remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce a unified framework that decomposes splitting-based reconstruction into these three components, enabling controlled comparison of existing methods and two incremental extensions: multi-partition splitting and an alternative inference strategy. Experiments on simulated LoDoPaB-CT data under independent and correlated noise, together with validation on the real-world 2DeteCT dataset, show that the optimal partitioning strategy strongly depends on the measurement noise structure. Lattice-based splitting performs favorably under independent noise, whereas angular masking is more robust under correlated noise and real measured data. Multi-partition splitting consistently improves over pure projection-wise splitting in several settings. Complementary perceptual and structural metrics, including LPIPS and HaarPSI, reveal differences between masking strategies that are less apparent from PSNR and SSIM alone. These results provide practical guidelines for designing self-supervised sparse-view CT reconstruction methods and highlight the limitations of common independence assumptions in realistic imaging environments.

</details>


### [105] [DP-Splat: Bayesian Nonparametric Complexity Control for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10912)
*Aqi Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将截断的stick-breaking狄利克雷过程先验（以及稀疏过拟合有限狄利克雷先验）引入3D高斯泼溅的变分贝叶斯框架，使得场景表示的组件数量K能够自适应数据复杂度，同时保持闭式坐标上升更新和自然梯度随机变分推断的高效性。实验表明该方法在合成数据和真实3D场景中均能自动选择恰当的组件数量，且比固定K的基线方法使用少5.9-7.6倍的组件达到相当或更优的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D高斯泼溅方法中，高斯组件的数量K要么通过启发式密度控制设定，要么由用户指定上限，缺乏自适应调整机制。变分贝叶斯高斯泼溅方法虽将问题转化为共轭变分推断，但K仍然是固定的。因此需要一种能够根据数据自动调整有效组件数量的方法。

Method: 将有限混合权重上的对称狄利克雷先验替换为：(1) 截断的stick-breaking狄利克雷过程先验；(2) 稀疏过拟合有限狄利克雷先验。保持每个更新步骤为闭式坐标上升步骤，并引入自然梯度随机变分推断使得每一步的计算成本与数据点数量无关。提供了精确的单调性保证、严格的截断误差界修正，并分析了拟合组件数量的估计含义。

Result: (i) 有效复杂度K̂能自适应场景复杂度，在分离良好的合成数据上恢复真实K（误差±1内）；(ii) DP先验的贡献在于复杂度选择而非组件效率——在匹配预算下DP拟合超过固定K基线+2.7dB，但收敛后两者持平；在3D场景中DP-Splat使用少5.9-7.6倍的组件达到或超过VBGS；(iii) 后验预测颜色方差在模型匹配的合成数据上校准良好；(iv) DP先验抵抗过度分裂，而稀疏有限混合会饱和其截断上限。

Conclusion: 狄利克雷过程先验能够有效实现组件数量的自适应选择，在减少冗余组件的同时保持或提升预测性能。同时揭示了变分实践与后验渐近理论之间的差距：在均值场坐标上升下，DP先验和有限混合的行为与精确后验渐近所暗示的顺序相反。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting represents scenes as finite mixtures of anisotropic Gaussians whose number of components $K$ is set by heuristic density control or user caps. Variational Bayes Gaussian Splatting (VBGS) recast splat fitting as conjugate variational inference, but $K$ remains fixed. We replace the finite symmetric Dirichlet over mixture weights with a truncated stick-breaking Dirichlet-process prior -- and, as a theory-backed alternative, a sparse overfitted finite Dirichlet -- so that the number of occupied components adapts to the data while every update remains a closed-form coordinate-ascent step; a natural-gradient stochastic variant makes the per-step cost independent of the number of points. We give an exact monotonicity guarantee, a rigorous truncation-error bound correcting an anti-conservative large-$α$ approximation in common use, and an honest account of what the fitted number of components estimates. Empirically: (i) the effective complexity $\hat{K}$ adapts to scene complexity and recovers the true $K$ within $\pm 1$ on well-separated synthetic data with regime-appropriate concentration; (ii) a deconfounded comparison shows the DP prior's contribution is complexity selection, not per-component efficiency -- converged DP fits exceed single-pass fixed-$K$ VBGS by +2.7 dB at matched budgets yet tie an equally converged fixed-$K$ baseline, and on 3D scenes DP-Splat matches or exceeds VBGS's held-out color prediction with 5.9-7.6x fewer components; (iii) the posterior-predictive color variance is well calibrated on model-matched synthetic data; and (iv) the ordering suggested by exact-posterior asymptotics reverses under mean-field coordinate ascent: the DP prior resists over-splitting while the sparse finite mixture saturates its truncation, a gap between variational practice and posterior asymptotics documented across three orders of magnitude in $N$.

</details>


### [106] [Unsupervised Detection of Entry and Exit Regions from Vehicle Trajectories for Camera-Agnostic Turning Movement Counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10949)
*Parikshit Singh Rathore,Vishwajeet Pattanaik,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无监督流水线，从原始车辆轨迹中自动识别交叉口的入口和出口区域，无需人工标注、相机标定或几何先验知识，在25个监控摄像头上中位分类误差约3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转向交通流计数对交叉口管理至关重要，但传统方法依赖每台相机的人工区域标注，成本高昂；现有轨迹聚类方法需要成对相似度计算且无法持续复用，缺乏高效、可迁移的无监督方案。

Method: 流水线包含六个步骤：对车辆轨迹的起始点和终止点进行空间聚类，生成持久化的入口/出口区域多边形；后续轨迹通过点-多边形包含测试以线性代价分类；通过19,152次流水线执行进行系统统计分析，识别三个显著参数并给出推荐配置。

Result: 在25个印度班加罗尔密集异质交通摄像头和UA-DETRAC基准上，推荐配置下中位分类误差约3%（含16个未见位置），GEH值满足工程阈值；相比轨迹聚类基线，稳定性更高、计算成本更低，但中位误差略高；≥60分钟校准片段和高峰时段选择可进一步提升质量。

Conclusion: 该无监督流水线有效降低了转向交通流计数的人工成本，具备良好的泛化能力和实用性，为大规模部署提供了经验支持。

Abstract: Turning movement counts are essential for intersection-level traffic management, yet their collection remains predominantly manual due to the cost of per-camera region annotation. This paper presents an unsupervised pipeline that identifies entry and exit regions directly from raw vehicle trajectories extracted via object detection and multi-object tracking, requiring no manual annotation, camera calibration, or prior knowledge of intersection geometry. Unlike trajectory clustering methods that classify individual trajectories using pairwise similarity and must be re-executed on every new batch, the proposed pipeline clusters initial and terminal point locations to produce persistent spatial region polygons that classify future trajectories by point-in-polygon containment at linear cost. The pipeline comprises six sequential steps, each with configurable parameters evaluated through a systematic statistical analysis spanning 19,152 pipeline executions across 25 surveillance cameras capturing dense heterogeneous traffic in Bengaluru, India, and 10 sequences from the UA-DETRAC benchmark dataset. Both parametric and nonparametric testing frameworks identify three consistently significant parameters and yield an empirically grounded recommended configuration. Under this configuration, the pipeline achieves a median classification error of approximately 3% across all 25 cameras, including 16 held-out locations, with GEH values within accepted engineering thresholds. Compared with two trajectory clustering baselines, the proposed pipeline exhibits greater stability across camera views and lower computational cost, at the expense of higher median error. Extended evaluation demonstrates that calibration clips of at least 60 minutes and peak-traffic selection further improve region estimation quality.

</details>


### [107] [Learning Anatomy-Grounded CT Vision-Language Representations with Organ-Hierarchical Report Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10953)
*Guoliang You,Hongming Li,Yuanwang Zhang,Yong Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OKA-CT提出了一种器官层级知识增强的CT-报告视觉语言预训练框架，通过解析放射学报告中的器官条件化知识，并在两阶段学习中分别注入解剖证据和进行结构化对比学习，在CT-RATE和RAD-ChestCT数据集上实现了先进的零样本异常诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VLP方法要么将整个扫描与完整报告对齐，要么将局部图像区域与文本片段对齐，未能充分利用放射学报告的一个关键特性——发现结果围绕解剖结构组织，异常描述包含器官、疾病概念、位置和严重程度属性。

Method: OKA-CT框架包含：(1) 通过放射学报告解析和LLM辅助语义结构化将自由文本报告转化为器官条件化知识；(2) 第一阶段通过细粒度器官条件化监督将解剖证据注入CT视觉表示；(3) 第二阶段利用器官特定报告证据引导结构化报告-CT对比学习，其中层级导出的语义软目标将共享器官级别发现的非配对样本视为弱语义正样本而非统一负样本；(4) 轻量级基于查询的全局分支聚合疾病相关体积证据用于全扫描表示。

Result: 在CT-RATE和RAD-ChestCT数据集上，OKA-CT零样本异常诊断AUROC分别达到84.9和72.2，优于先前CT VLP基线。检索和补丁遮挡分析显示改进的报告-图像对齐和对疾病相关解剖区域的更强敏感性。

Conclusion: OKA-CT通过器官层级知识增强和两阶段学习有效利用了放射学报告的结构化特性，显著提升了CT-报告视觉语言预训练的性能和对解剖区域的敏感性。

Abstract: Medical vision-language pretraining (VLP) from paired CT images and radiology reports enables scalable representation learning, but most existing methods align either whole scans with entire reports or local image regions with text fragments. These formulations underuse a key property of radiology reports: findings are organized around anatomical structures, with abnormalities described by organs, disease concepts, locations, and severity-related attributes. We propose OKA-CT, an organ-hierarchical knowledge-augmented framework for CT-report VLP. OKA-CT first converts free-text reports into organ-conditioned knowledge using radiology report parsing and LLM-assisted semantic structuring. The extracted hierarchy is used across two learning stages. Stage~1 injects anatomy-grounded evidence into the CT visual representation through fine-grained organ-conditioned supervision, while Stage~2 uses organ-specific report evidence to guide structured report-CT contrastive learning, where hierarchy-derived semantic soft targets treat non-paired cases with shared organ-level findings as weak semantic positives rather than uniform negatives. A lightweight query-based global branch further aggregates disease-relevant volumetric evidence for whole-scan representation. On CT-RATE and RAD-ChestCT datasets, OKA-CT achieves zero-shot abnormality diagnosis AUROCs of 84.9 and 72.2, outperforming prior CT VLP baselines. Retrieval and patch-occlusion analyses further show improved report-image alignment and stronger sensitivity to disease-associated anatomical regions.

</details>


### [108] [EquiFusion: Kinematics-Agnostic Human Motion Prediction via Equivariant Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10984)
*Cecilia Curreli,Florian Hofherr,Dominik Muhle,Abhishek Saroha,Riccardo Marin,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EquiFusion 是首个运动学无关的随机3D人体运动预测模型，采用置换等变架构的潜在扩散模型，将骨骼连接作为显式输入参数，实现了跨数据集的泛化、零样本预测（如遮挡部分预测和肢体生成），在主流基准上达到SOTA，模型体积缩小75%，训练和推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机3D人体运动预测模型硬编码骨骼运动学结构（即关节连接顺序和图结构），严重限制了模型的泛化能力，无法进行跨数据集训练，且需要复杂的数据重定向处理，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出 EquiFusion，采用置换等变架构的潜在扩散模型（latent diffusion model），将运动学连接作为显式输入参数，使内部计算天然地与关节顺序和图结构无关，实现运动学无关的预测。

Result: 在主流基准上达到SOTA，模型体积比现有运动学特定方法缩小75%，训练和推理速度更快，支持跨数据集泛化及零样本任务（遮挡观测预测、目标肢体生成）。

Conclusion: EquiFusion 建立了灵活、鲁棒的人体运动预测新标准，突破了运动学硬编码的限制。

Abstract: Existing Stochastic 3D Human Motion Prediction models are fundamentally constrained by hard-coding the skeleton kinematics, severely limiting generalization, preventing cross-dataset training, and requiring complex data retargeting. We introduce EquiFusion, the first kinematics-agnostic model to solve this bottleneck, implementing a latent diffusion model with a permutation equivariant architecture. EquiFusion treats the kinematics' connectivity as an explicit input parameter, ensuring its internal computations are inherently agnostic to joint ordering and graph structure. This novel design enables truly cross-dataset generalization to unseen kinematics and unlocks novel zero-shot directions, such as motion prediction from partial or occluded observations and targeted limb generation. EquiFusion achieves state-of-the-art results on major benchmarks, being up to 75% more compact than previous kinematics-specific methods, while achieving faster training and inference. EquiFusion thus establishes a new, flexible standard for robust human motion prediction. Model and training code are available at https://ceveloper.github.io/publications/equifusion/.

</details>


### [109] [MED-DSLC: Multi-Expert-Domain Classification via Domain Supervision and Logit Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10985)
*Zheng Zeng,Deepak Sridhar,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MED-DSLC方法，通过域监督训练和域级logit缩放，解决多专家领域分类中LoRA合并后logit全局不可比导致的跨域干扰问题，在细粒度基准上平均准确率提升15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs（如CLIP）通过LoRA微调提升域内精度，但会导致域外精度下降，产生大量碎片化专家模型。多专家领域分类通过合并独立训练的LoRA来缓解此问题，但专家间logit尺度不一致引发跨域干扰，导致高置信度错误预测。

Method: 提出MED-DSLC：1) 域监督训练（Domain Supervised Training），在联合域类集上训练以显式对齐logit尺度；2) 域级logit缩放（Domain-wise Logit Scaling），通过轻量缩放因子补偿不同域间的logit幅度差异，恢复全局可比性。

Result: 在多个细粒度基准上，MED-DSLC相比基线平均准确率提升15%，显著增强跨域鲁棒性和可扩展性，尤其在高度数据不平衡场景下效果突出。

Conclusion: 恢复输出级校准是实现多域专业化下真正零样本VLM的关键，MED-DSLC以最小数据代价有效解决了跨域logit干扰问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP enable zero-shot classification by comparing image features with text prompts in a shared embedding space. A fundamental property underlying this capability is the global comparability of logits across arbitrary candidate classes. However, VLMs are often adapted to fine-grained domains using techniques such as LoRA. While this improves in-domain accuracy, out-of-domain accuracy degrades. This leads to a highly fragmented model ecosystem, with thousands of specialized models. Multi-Expert-Domain classification seeks to address this problem, by merging LoRAs trained independently on specialized domains. However, due to the independent training, the various domain experts no longer produce globally calibrated logits. As a result, when evaluating over the union of multiple domain-specific class sets, heterogeneous logit scales induce cross-domain interference and artificially high confidence for out-of-domain classes, inducing prediction errors. In this work, we identify domain supervision and cross-domain logit miscalibration as the key issue to scalable multi-domain zero-shot recognition. We propose MED-DSLC, combining domain supervised training and domain-wise logit scaling, to explicitly restore global logit comparability. MED-DSLC is a lightweight solution for MED classification, which is shown to preserve within-domain discrimination while reducing cross-domain logit interference with minimal data. Extensive experiments across diverse fine-grained benchmarks demonstrate that it substantially improves mean accuracy (+15\%), cross-domain robustness, and scalability in the size of MED classification problem. Our results show that restoring output-level calibration is essential under highly data imbalanced settings for achieving a truly zero-shot VLM under multi-domain specialization.

</details>


### [110] [MMA-Former: Multi-Window Mixture-of-Head Attention Transformer for Adaptive PNI Prediction in 3D MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10988)
*Youngung Han,Induk Um,Kyeonghun Kim,Junga Kim,Hyunsu Go,Jaewon Jung,Woo Kyoung Jeong,Won Jae Lee,Pa Hong,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MMA-Former，一种基于多窗口混合头注意力的3D Transformer架构，用于从MRI中非侵入性预测胆管癌的神经侵犯（PNI），在168例扫描中达到AUC 0.752，优于CNN和标准Transformer基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PNI是胆管癌的重要预后因素，但现有方法难以从3D MRI中同时捕获细粒度细节和全局上下文进行非侵入性预测。

Method: 提出MMA-Former，包含粗-细Transformer（CFT）结构实现多尺度特征提取，并创新性地引入窗口特定混合头注意力（WS-MoH），为每个3D窗口动态分配专门或共享注意力头，实现空间自适应特征提取。

Result: 在168例T1加权MRI回顾性数据集上，MMA-Former的AUC为0.752，超过最佳CNN（0.708）和最佳Transformer基线（0.681）。

Conclusion: WS-MoH机制在不增加参数的情况下增强了特征提取的专门化并减少了冗余，使MMA-Former能有效从3D MRI中预测PNI。

Abstract: Perineural invasion (PNI) is a critical prognostic factor in cholangiocarcinoma. Non-invasive prediction from 3D MRI is challenging, demanding models that efficiently capture both fine-grained details and global context. We propose the Multi-window Mixture-of-Head Attention Transformer (MMA-Former), a novel end-to-end 3D architecture featuring a Coarse-Fine Transformer (CFT) structure for parallel multi-scale feature extraction. We advance this structure by integrating a novel Window-Specific Mixture-of-Head attention (WS-MoH) mechanism. Unlike standard Multi-Head Self Attention (MSA), WS-MoH generates a representation for each 3D window and dynamically routes the entire window to specialized or common attention heads. This enables spatially adaptive feature extraction tailored to the local context of each window, enhancing specialization and reducing redundancy without increasing parameters. Evaluated on a retrospective dataset of 168 T1-weighted MRI scans, MMA-Former achieved an AUC of 0.752, outperforming other 3D architectures, including the best CNN (AUC of 0.708) and Transformer baselines (AUC of 0.681).

</details>


### [111] [TreeSoc: Tree-Structured Dynamic Reasoning and Tool Synergy for Soccer Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10990)
*Thanh-Nhan Vo,Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Trung-Hoang Le,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TreeSoc提出了一种结构化推理框架，将足球视频问答重构为层次化搜索问题，通过动态深度优先搜索机制分解复杂查询，结合自适应工具路由，在SoccerBench和NExT-QA上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言模型在理解复杂足球场景时存在浅层跨模态对齐、多步推理能力不足以及工具集成受限等根本性问题。

Method: 提出TreeSoc框架，采用动态深度优先搜索（DFS）机制将复杂查询分解为有序子任务，通过树状结构支持迭代推理优化和自适应工具路由，选择性激活领域特定模块并在每个推理节点整合其输出。

Result: 在SoccerBench上达到TextQA 85.2%、ImageQA 87.4%、VideoQA 82.2%的准确率；在NExT-QA上达到74.16%准确率，均达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 结构化的、工具增强的树推理范式是鲁棒视频理解的有效方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated understanding of complex soccer scenarios from video remains a significant challenge for contemporary vision-language models (VLMs), which suffer from shallow cross-modal alignment and exhibit fundamental limitations in multi-step reasoning and coordinated tool integration. We present TreeSoc, a structured reasoning framework that reformulates soccer video question answering as a hierarchical search problem rather than a single-pass prediction. Specifically, TreeSoc employs a dynamic depth-first search (DFS) mechanism that decomposes complex queries into sequentially ordered sub-tasks, enabling iterative reasoning refinement through explicit intermediate states. This tree-structured decomposition naturally supports adaptive tool routing, wherein domain-specific modules are selectively activated and their outputs incorporated at each reasoning node to produce contextually grounded predictions. On SoccerBench, TreeSoc achieves state-of-the-art performance, with accuracies of 85.2%, 87.4%, and 82.2% on TextQA, ImageQA, and VideoQA, respectively. Additionally, TreeSoc further demonstrates strong cross-domain generalization, attaining 74.16% accuracy on NExT-QA. These results establish structured, tool-augmented tree reasoning as an effective paradigm for robust video understanding. Code is available at: https://github.com/thanhnhan29/TreeSoc.

</details>


### [112] [LoSA-Net: A Localized and Scale-Adaptive Network for Boundary-Sensitive Prediction of Perineural Invasion in 3D MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10992)
*Youngung Han,Hyunsu Go,Kyeonghun Kim,Induk Um,Junga Kim,Jaewon Jung,Woo Kyoung Jeong,Won Jae Lee,Pa Hong,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoSA-Net是一种用于3D MRI中周围神经侵犯（PNI）术前预测的局部化尺度自适应架构，通过Talking Neighborhood Attention (TNA)、Scale-Adaptive Feature Mixing (SAFM)和Cross-Scale Refinement and Alignment (CSRA)三个核心模块，在168例胆管癌患者增强MRI扫描中达到AUC 0.7567，优于现有卷积和Transformer基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周围神经侵犯（PNI）是肿瘤侵袭性的临床相关指标，影响手术决策，但其在MRI上的细微特征常与邻近解剖结构相似，而常规下采样或全局特征聚合会削弱这些微细的神经周围线索，使得现有三维体积模型在术前无创预测方面效果不佳。

Method: 提出了LoSA-Net架构，包含三个核心模块：(1) Talking Neighborhood Attention (TNA) — 通过局部化自注意力与多头混合机制保留神经对齐的细节；(2) Scale-Adaptive Feature Mixing (SAFM) — 利用多尺度深度可分离处理调节感受野；(3) Cross-Scale Refinement and Alignment (CSRA) — 在不同阶段间维持语义上下文与高分辨率边界的一致性。

Result: 在168例胆管癌患者的增强MRI扫描中，LoSA-Net达到AUC 0.7567，在匹配的预处理和优化设置下优于代表性的卷积和Transformer基线模型。

Conclusion: LoSA-Net通过局部化和尺度自适应的设计有效解决了PNI细微特征在常规模型中容易被衰减的问题，为术前无创PNI预测提供了可行的深度学习方案。

Abstract: Perineural invasion (PNI) is a clinically relevant indicator of tumor aggressiveness and can influence surgical decision-making, motivating interest in reliable preoperative assessment. The subtle MRI features of PNI, however, often resemble nearby anatomy, complicating noninvasive prediction. These fine perineural cues are easily attenuated by routine downsampling or overly global feature aggregation, reducing the effectiveness of conventional volumetric models. We present LoSA-Net, a localized and scale-adaptive architecture for boundary-sensitive PNI prediction in 3D MRI. Talking Neighborhood Attention (TNA) preserves nerve-aligned detail through localized self-attention with head-wise mixing, and Scale-Adaptive Feature Mixing (SAFM) modulates the receptive field using multi-scale depthwise processing. Cross-Scale Refinement and Alignment (CSRA) maintains consistency between semantic context and high-resolution boundaries across stages. In contrast-enhanced MRI scans from 168 patients with cholangiocarcinoma, LoSA-Net achieves an AUC of 0.7567 and outperforms representative convolutional and transformer baselines under matched preprocessing and optimization settings.

</details>


### [113] [Confidence Scores in Open-Vocabulary Detection Are a Biased Mixture of Scale and Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10993)
*Yi Tang Soon,Jun-Wei Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验揭示了基于CLIP等基础模型构建的开放词汇目标检测器（GroundingDINO、OWL-ViT、YOLO-World）的置信度分数存在两种结构性偏差：尺度偏差（大物体得分系统性偏高）和语义偏差（通用查询得分被抑制）。这些偏差源于CLIP的图像级预训练，无法通过阈值调整消除。作者提出一种无参数的温度缩放校正方法，在不重新训练的情况下将小物体Recall@10提升19.6%，但会轻微降低排序精度，表明偏差仅能部分逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇目标检测器（如基于CLIP的模型）虽能泛化到新类别，但其置信度分数并非可靠的位置概率估计，混淆了视觉尺度和语义查询特异性与真实检测信号。本文旨在揭示并量化这一根本性偏差，并探索其可逆性。

Method: 在COCO数据集上对三种基础模型检测器（GroundingDINO、OWL-ViT、YOLO-World）进行控制实验，并在LVIS（1,203类）上用GroundingDINO复现尺度偏差。通过统计回归量化尺度偏差（α=+0.064, r=0.579, p=1.29×10⁻⁵⁸）和语义偏差（β=-0.705, p=5.23×10⁻⁴¹）。提出无参数的温度缩放校正方法，并评估oracle每尺度阈值调整的效果。

Result: 尺度偏差系统性抬高了大物体的分数，语义偏差抑制了通用查询的分数。Oracle每尺度阈值调整对F1提升极小（小物体+0.001 vs 大物体+0.102）。提出的温度缩放校正将小物体Recall@10提升19.6%（p＜0.01），但以轻微降低池化排序精度为代价。

Conclusion: 将图像级基础模型适配到区域级检测任务存在根本性局限——置信度偏差源于CLIP预训练范式，且仅能部分逆转。这揭示了开放词汇检测器置信度校准的固有困难。

Abstract: Foundation models such as CLIP have enabled open-vocabulary object detectors that generalise to novel categories via vision-language similarity. However, the confidence scores these detectors produce are not reliable localization probability estimates: they conflate visual scale and semantic query specificity with the true detection signal. Through controlled experiments on COCO across three foundation-model-based detectors (GroundingDINO, OWL-ViT, YOLO-World), with the scale-bias finding further replicated on LVIS (1,203 categories) using GroundingDINO, we show that s=cos(v,t) is a biased mixture of two effects. Scale bias (alpha = +0.064, r = 0.579, p = 1.29 x 10^-58) systematically inflates scores for large objects. Semantic bias (beta = -0.705, p = 5.23 x 10^-41) suppresses scores for generic queries. Both biases are structurally inevitable from CLIP's image-level pretraining. Threshold adjustment cannot remove them: oracle per-scale thresholding yields Delta F1 = +0.001 for small objects versus +0.102 for large. A parameter-free temperature scaling correction improves small-object Recall@10 by 19.6% (p < 0.01) without retraining. This comes at a modest, measurable cost to pooled-ranking precision, so the bias is partially, not freely, reversible at inference time. These findings reveal a fundamental limitation of adapting image-level foundation models to region-level detection tasks.

</details>


### [114] [AsySplat: Efficient Asymmetric 3D Gaussian Splatting for Long-Sequence Scene Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10995)
*Yingji Zhong,Dave Zhenyu Chen,Fuzhao Ou,Youyu Chen,Zhihao Li,Lanqing Hong,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非对称架构，将几何和外观建模解耦：几何分支用大部分参数处理粗粒度token进行多视图重建，外观分支用少量参数处理细粒度token捕捉细节，通过双边连接交互。在32视图960P输入上，该模型匹配基于优化的方法并实现近800倍加速，以更少参数和训练/推理成本超越最先进的泛化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可泛化3D高斯泼溅模型在长序列新视角合成中存在大量冗余计算。作者基于两个观察：(i)高精度几何对高质量NVS并非严格必要；(ii)外观学习通常比几何恢复更容易，因此希望减少计算冗余，更合理地分配计算资源。

Method: 提出非对称架构，将几何与外观建模解耦。几何分支：处理粗粒度token，分配大部分参数进行多视图重建；外观分支：处理细粒度token，使用显著更少的参数捕捉细节；两分支通过双边连接交互，实现各自任务的相互指导。这种任务感知的非对称性减少了计算冗余，提高了参数效率。

Result: 在32视图960P输入上，模型匹配基于优化的方法，同时实现近800倍加速；超越最先进可泛化模型的零样本性能，且参数更少、训练/推理开销更低。

Conclusion: 任务感知的非对称架构能有效减少计算冗余，更合理地分配计算资源，从而以更小的模型实现强性能，在效率上实现整体提升。

Abstract: Recent generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting models have advanced long-sequence novel view synthesis (NVS), but at the cost of substantial redundant computation. We identify that the redundancy can be mitigated based on two observations: (i) high-precision geometry is not strictly required for high-quality NVS; (ii) appearance learning is generally easier than geometry recovery. Motivated by these insights, we propose an asymmetric architecture that decouples geometry and appearance modeling. The geometry branch processes coarse-grained tokens with most of the parameters for multi-view reconstruction, while the appearance branch operates on fine-grained tokens to capture details using significantly fewer parameters. The two branches interact through bilateral connections, enabling mutual guidance for their respective tasks. This task-aware asymmetry reduces the computational redundancy and allocates the computation more judiciously, thereby increasing parameter efficiency and enabling smaller models to achieve strong performance. On 32-view 960P inputs, our model matches optimization-based methods while delivering nearly 800x speedup, and surpasses the zero-shot performance of state-of-the-art generalizable models with markedly fewer parameters and reduced training/inference overhead, achieving an overall efficiency improvement.

</details>


### [115] [Simple Features and Honest Calibration for Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11120)
*Vikas Kumar,Aditya Mishra,Haroon R. Lone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文面向ABAW 2026 BAH挑战中的矛盾/犹豫(A/H)识别任务，提出了一种融合情感专用文本、音频和视觉表征，并结合语言犹豫线索的系统。核心创新包括ASR擦除时间特征（利用填充词和犹豫停顿的时间间隙构建16维特征，AP达0.718）和情感标记融合(AMF)可靠性门控机制。实验发现：跨模态冲突设计无帮助，语言模态最强，音频次之，校准比架构更重要。最终采用固定阈值的AP加权集成，在测试集上达到0.731 macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矛盾/犹豫识别方法缺乏对跨模态信息的有效融合，且忽视语音识别中填充词和犹豫停顿的时间结构信息。

Method: 1) 构建ASR擦除时间特征：利用语音识别删除填充词/犹豫停顿后的时间间隙，提取16维特征；2) 提出情感标记融合(AMF)：基于可靠性的门控机制融合文本、音频、视觉表征及语言犹豫线索；3) 采用AP加权集成与固定决策阈值。

Result: ASR擦除时间特征AP达0.718，与其他通道相关性仅0.11-0.36，证明其独立性强；最终系统在测试集上达到0.731 macro-F1。

Conclusion: 语言模态在A/H识别中占主导地位，情感专用音频是有效的辅助通道；校准比架构设计更重要；在有限验证集上拟合权重和阈值会导致过拟合，AP加权固定阈值策略更鲁棒。

Abstract: We address ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) recognition in the ABAW 2026 BAH Challenge: given a short interview video, predict whether the person shows signs of A/H. Our system combines affect-specialised text, audio, and visual representations with a small set of readable linguistic hesitation cues, fused by a reliability gate we call Affective Marker Fusion (AMF), and finished with a simple AP-weighted ensemble at a fixed decision threshold. We also introduce \emph{ASR-erased time}: speech recognisers delete fillers and hesitation pauses from the transcript, but the chunk timestamps keep the time those events took, and sixteen features built from these gaps form the strongest and most independent non-verbal channel we measured (AP $0.718$, correlation $0.11$--$0.36$ with all other members). Across controlled experiments we find three things: cross-modal conflict design does not reliably help on BAH; language is by far the strongest channel while affect-specialised audio is a useful second; and calibration matters more than architecture. Fitting ensemble weights and a threshold on the small validation split overfits: it scores $0.741$ macro-F1 on validation but only $0.690$ on the untouched test set. AP-weighting at a fixed threshold instead reaches $\mathbf{0.731}$ on test.

</details>


### [116] [Temporal Feature Distillation for Label-Efficient Precise Event Spotting in Sports Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10998)
*Hao Xu,Xinyu Wei,Sam Wells,Sunil Aryal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出时序特征蒸馏（Temporal Feature Distillation）和Transformer门控移位模块（Transformer Gate Shift），用于精确事件定位（PES）。该方法在半监督设置下，通过对骨干网络特征而非投影头输出进行对齐，保留运动敏感和边界感知线索，结合监督预热和渐进式调度，有效提升视频帧级事件定位性能。在四个细粒度体育基准上，10%标注数据下mAP提升4.54点，80%标注数据即可匹配或超越全监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确事件定位（PES）需要区分视觉相似但语义不同的相邻帧，这与图像分类和粗粒度动作识别本质不同。现有自蒸馏方法（如DINO）直接应用于PES效果不佳：缺乏监督引导时，细微但关键的运动线索常被作为噪声抑制，导致表征对精确事件边界不敏感。

Method: 1) 时序特征蒸馏（Temporal Feature Distillation）：一种半监督目标，对齐时序信息丰富的骨干网络特征（而非投影头输出），以保留运动敏感和边界感知线索用于帧级定位；2) 监督预热+渐进式调度（ramp-up schedule）：在无标签蒸馏开始前确保有意义的事件线索被学习，稳定训练过程；3) Transformer门控移位（Transformer Gate Shift）：一种多尺度门控移位模块，将运动感知时序信息注入Vision Transformer。

Result: 在四个细粒度体育基准上，该方法一致优于全监督和半监督基线。在FSPerf上仅用10%标注数据，mAP提升4.54点；仅用80%标注数据，即可在四个数据集中的两个上匹配或超越全监督100%标注的基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的时序特征蒸馏和Transformer门控移位模块有效解决了自蒸馏方法在精确事件定位中运动信息被抑制的问题，在半监督设定下显著提升了帧级事件定位性能，减少了标注依赖。

Abstract: Precise Event Spotting (PES) requires distinguishing visually similar yet semantically distinct adjacent frames, making it fundamentally different from image classification and coarse action recognition. Although self-distillation methods such as DINO have shown strong representation learning ability in images, we find that directly applying them to PES is ineffective: without supervised guidance, subtle but crucial motion cues are often suppressed as noise, leading to representations that are insensitive to precise event boundaries. To address this, we propose Temporal Feature Distillation, a semi-supervised objective that aligns temporally informative backbone features, rather than projection-head outputs, to preserve motion-sensitive and boundary-aware cues for frame-level localization. A supervised warm-up with a ramp-up schedule further stabilizes training by ensuring that meaningful event cues are learned before unlabeled distillation begins. We also introduce Transformer Gate Shift, a multi-scale gated shifting module that injects motion-aware temporal information into Vision Transformers. Experiments on four fine-grained sports benchmarks show consistent improvements over fully supervised and semi-supervised baselines. Under 10\% supervision on FSPerf, our method improves mAP by 4.54 points over the strongest competing approach, and with only 80\% labeled data, it matches or surpasses the fully supervised 100\% baseline on two of the four datasets.

</details>


### [117] [SynCLIP: Synonym-Coherent Language-Image Pretraining for Robust Open-Vocabulary Dense Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11008)
*Mingjie Xie,Guangjun He,Dongli Xu,Youtian Lin,Hongjue Li,Pengming Feng,Jian Guan,Yue Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SynCLIP通过语义一致的空间注意力对齐（SSA）和空间注意力细化（SAR）模块，解决开放词汇密集感知中同义词导致的空间注意力不一致问题，并构建了同义词增强视觉语料库SEViC，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP-based开放词汇密集感知方法存在同义词引发的定位不一致问题：语义等价的表达会产生不同的空间注意力模式，严重影响模型在真实场景中的鲁棒性和性能。

Method: 提出SynCLIP框架，包含：(1) 语义一致空间注意力对齐（SSA）模块，通过最小化原始表达与同义表达注意力图之间的差异来增强空间注意力一致性；(2) 空间注意力细化（SAR）模块，选择性增强对齐图中最相关的语义区域以获得更精确的定位；(3) 构建同义词增强视觉语料库SEViC，为每个类别扩充多个同义词和文本定义。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，SynCLIP显著提升了在多种语言变体下的定位一致性，并在CLIP-based开放词汇密集感知方法中达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SynCLIP有效解决了同义词引发的空间注意力不一致问题，为开放词汇密集感知提供了更鲁棒的对齐方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary dense perception (OVDP) aims to localize objects unseen during training by leveraging textual knowledge. Despite the remarkable progress of recent CLIP-based approaches, we identify a critical limitation: synonym-induced grounding inconsistency, where semantically equivalent expressions yield disparate spatial attention patterns. This inconsistency undermines the robustness and performance of existing methods in real-world OVDP applications. To address this issue, we propose SynCLIP, a Synonym-Coherent Language-Image Pretraining framework that enhances synonym-robust grounding for OVDP. SynCLIP introduces a Semantic-consistent Spatial Attention alignment (SSA) module to enhance spatial attention consistency by minimizing discrepancies between attention maps of original and synonymous expressions. Furthermore, a Spatial Attention Refinement (SAR) module selectively strengthens the most semantically relevant spatial regions within aligned maps for more precise and stable grounding. To support synonym-coherent pretraining, we also construct a Synonym-Enriched Visual Corpus (SEViC), which augments each category with multiple synonyms and textual definitions. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SynCLIP substantially improves grounding consistency under diverse linguistic variants and achieves state-of-the-art performance among CLIP-based OVDP methods. Code is available at https://github.com/Justlovesmile/SynCLIP.

</details>


### [118] [Reference-Based Face Super-Resolution Using the Spatial Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11025)
*Varun Ramesh Jois,Antonella DiLillo,James Storer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于参考图像的人脸超分辨率方法（FSRST），使用空间变换器进行对齐，并设计了自适应的信息聚合函数，在多个数据集上达到了SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸超分辨率是一个病态问题，现有方法在保持高保真度方面存在挑战，用户往往意识不到该任务被使用但对其质量有较高要求。

Method: (1) 基于空间变换器（Spatial Transformer）的对齐模块，比可变形卷积更稳定；(2) 自适应聚合函数，能在参考图像质量好时提取信息，质量差时抑制其作用；(3) 整体模型较小但仍能达到SOTA。

Result: 在多个数据集上取得了最先进的结果（state of the art）。

Conclusion: 基于参考图像的人脸超分辨率方案通过更稳定的对齐模块和自适应聚合机制，可以用较小的模型获得更好的超分效果。

Abstract: Face super-resolution is the task of increasing the resolution of an image containing a face thereby adding finer detail. It is a ubiquitous task in many computer vision applications and quite often the user isn't even aware that it is being performed. However, doing it with high fidelity is challenging as it is an ill-posed problem. In this paper we present a reference-based solution for face super-resolution that uses higher resolution reference images to aid in the task. We show an alignment module based on the spatial transformer that is considerably more stable than the popular deformable convolutions. We also show an aggregation function that can take good quality information from the reference images when available or suppress the function when such information is unavailable. Finally, we show that our relatively smaller model can achieve state of the art results on multiple datasets. The source code is available at https://github.com/varun-jois/FSRST.

</details>


### [119] [RTFVE: Realtime Face Video Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11034)
*Varun Ramesh Jois,Antonella DiLillo,James Storer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RTFVE模型，一种可在普通CPU上实时运行、易于集成到任何视频解码器中的面部视频增强方法，在多个低码率设置下显著提升压缩视频的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频会议应用广泛使用，但许多用户面临带宽限制，导致低码率视频质量下降。现有深度学习增强模型难以与现代压缩标准集成，或需要专用GPU硬件，缺乏实用性。

Method: 提出RTFVE（Realtime Face Video Enhancement）模型，可轻松集成到任何视频解码器中，在普通CPU上实现实时运行。

Result: 在多个低码率设置下，RTFVE模型相比压缩视频基线显著提升了感知质量。

Conclusion: RTFVE是一种实用、高效的低码率面部视频增强方案，解决了现有模型在兼容性和硬件需求上的局限，代码将开源。

Abstract: There's been a surge in adoption of video conferencing applications for both personal and business use cases. However, the bandwidth limitations faced by many users worldwide may restrict the optimal use of such applications. Although deep learning offers a solution for enhancing low bit rate videos, most models today are either hard to incorporate with modern compression standards or require specialized hardware to run such as significant GPUs making these models impractical.
