This design enables targeted evidence acquisition, preserves sparse cross-modal cues, and provides inspectable control over repair and stopping. Omni-Decision achieves 45.6% accuracy on OmniGAIA and 58.3% on WorldSense, improving over the baselines by +27.3 and +30.2 percentage points, respectively. No-state ablations and trajectory audits further support the role of explicit evidence-state control in multi-step omni-modal evidence seeking.

</details>


### [386] [The Ebb and Flow of Multimodal Focus: Scheduling Visual Relay Windows for Grounded VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11436)
*Wencheng Ye,Yi Bin,Yujuan Ding,Hongye Fang,Zheng Wang,Xing Xu,Jingkuan Song,Yun Zhang,Sirui Da,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过机械论视角揭示了VLM中多模态注意力存在稳定的三阶段重分配模式，识别出关键的"视觉中继窗口"(VRW)，并提出任务自适应推理时控制框架TRACE，通过轻量模块在预填充和解码阶段重塑注意力分配，显著提升了基于证据的多模态推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在多模态推理基准上表现提升，但其视觉证据进入语言栈后变得不稳定，削弱了基于证据的推理能力。现有方法缺乏对VLM内部注意力动态的理解，需要从机制层面揭示其运作规律。

Method: 1) 通过机械论分析揭示VLM内部存在稳定的三阶段注意力重分配：早期问题条件组织、中期视觉主导中继、晚期答案形成。2) 将中期阶段定义为视觉中继窗口(VRW)，证明其几何结构随任务需求变化、与有依据生成存在因果联系、能区分无支撑答案与强推理轨迹。3) 提出TRACE框架，包含轻量训练模块，在预填充阶段重塑中继分配，在解码阶段保持组装后的视觉支持。

Result: 在4个开源VLM和7个基准上，TRACE在接地敏感设置上平均提升4.33个点，最高提升6.6个点，同时推理密集型任务也有改进。

Conclusion: 明确控制深度方向上多模态注意力焦点为加强基于证据的多模态推理提供了统一且有效的机制。

Abstract: Vision-language models increasingly succeed on multimodal reasoning benchmarks, yet their visual evidence often becomes unstable once it enters the language stack, weakening evidence-grounded reasoning. To understand this fragility, we examine the internal dynamics of VLMs through a mechanistic lens and uncover a stable three-stage redistribution of multimodal attention focus across depth: an early question-conditioned organization, a critical middle visual-dominant relay, and a late return to answer formation. We operationalize the middle phase as the Visual Relay Window (VRW), and show that its geometry varies with task demand, is causally tied to grounded generation, and distinguishes unsupported answers from stronger reasoning trajectories. Guided by this internal rhythm, we propose TRACE, a task-adaptive inference-time control framework with lightweight trained modules. It reshapes relay allocation during prefill and preserves assembled visual support after handoff during decoding. Across four open-weight VLM backbones and seven benchmarks, TRACE delivers large gains on grounding-sensitive settings, improving them by 4.33 points on average and by up to 6.6 points, while also improving reasoning-heavy tasks. These results show that explicitly controlling multimodal focus across depth offers a unified and effective mechanism for strengthening evidence-grounded multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [387] [Enhancing Query Efficiency for d-DNNF Representations Through Preprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11492)
*Jean Marie Lagniez,Emmanuel Lonca*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向CNF公式模型访问（均匀采样、直接访问、模型枚举）的预处理技术，发现大多数不保持等价性的预处理器不适用，而保持模型计数并保留预处理信息的预处理器可有效提升d-DNNF编译后的查询性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CNF公式预处理器大多针对SAT求解优化，不保持公式等价性；而模型采样、直接访问和枚举等任务需要保持模型空间的完整性，现有预处理方案在这些任务上缺乏系统性的研究。

Method: 系统分析不同预处理技术对模型访问任务的影响，识别出保持模型计数且保留预处理信息的关键条件；在此基础上设计预处理方法，并在多个领域的基准测试集上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的预处理方法高效且鲁棒，在将CNF公式编译为d-DNNF表示后，显著提升了模型访问查询的性能。

Conclusion: 保持模型计数的预处理器结合预处理信息的保留，是提升CNF公式模型访问效率的有效途径，尤其适用于d-DNNF编译场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate preprocessing techniques aimed at improving the efficiency of accessing models of propositional formulas represented in conjunctive normal form (CNF). We focus on three fundamental tasks: uniform sampling, direct model access, and model enumeration. Our analysis reveals that most state-of-the-art preprocessors, when they do not preserve formula equivalence, are generally unsuitable for these tasks. In contrast, we demonstrate that preprocessors which preserve model counts can be effectively leveraged, provided relevant preprocessing information is maintained. To validate our approach, we perform extensive experiments on a diverse suite of benchmarks from multiple domains. The experimental results show that our preprocessing methods are both efficient and robust, yielding significant performance improvements for model access queries when CNF formulas are compiled into d-DNNF representations.

</details>


### [388] [Comparative Analysis of GAT and BERT for Human-Like Playtesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11501)
*Kleio Fragkedaki,Theodoros Panagiotakopoulos,Matteo Biasielli,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer（BERT）和图注意力网络（GAT）的通用架构来建模玩家游戏体验，在《糖果粉碎传奇》中相比CNN基线方法表现出更优性能，且减少了特征工程维护需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的游戏玩法测试模型难以捕捉完整玩家策略，且需要大量特征工程和网络架构建模；当引入新游戏机制或功能时，模型需要持续调整。为此，本文旨在提出一种更通用的表示方法，减少甚至消除持续的特征工程维护需求。

Method: 研究了两种通用网络架构：(a)基于Transformer的BERT模型和(b)图注意力模型GAT，两者均设计用于有效捕捉《糖果粉碎传奇》游戏棋盘的 relational structure。实验将这两种方法与卷积神经网络CNN基线进行对比。

Result: 在具有挑战性的棋盘配置上，BERT和GAT相比CNN基线方法取得了更优的性能表现，验证了通用化表示方法的优势。

Conclusion: 提出的通用网络架构能够有效捕捉游戏棋盘的关系结构，减少特征工程维护，并在复杂游戏场景中表现更好，为设计更具吸引力的解谜游戏提供了新的数据驱动方法。

Abstract: Accurately modeling and understanding player experience is crucial for designing engaging puzzle games. To achieve this, a common approach involves collecting diverse user data to train predictive playtesting models that mimic player behavior. However, existing data-driven methods often lack the ability to capture the full range of player strategies and require extensive feature engineering and network architecture modeling. This limitation becomes particularly evident when new game mechanics or features are introduced, which necessitate continual adjustments to the models. To addrss these challenges, we propose a more generalized representation that reduces - or even eliminates - the need for ongoing feature-engineering maintenance. Specifically, we investigate two general-purpose network architectures: (a) a transformer-based model (BERT) and (b) a graph attention model (GAT), both of which are designed to effectively capture the relational structure of Candy Crush Saga (CCS) game boards. Our experiments compare these approaches to Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) baselines, revealing better performance on challenging board configurations and underscoring the benefits of our generalizable representation.

</details>


### [389] [Learning Residual Kinematic Corrections for Continuous Neural Decoding via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11530)
*Jiamian Li,Niall McShane,Attila Korik,Naomi du Bois,Karl McCreadie,Leen Jabban,Benjamin Metcalfe,Özgür Şimşek,Damien Coyle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段解码框架，在CNN-LSTM解码器后引入强化学习（RL）进行残差运动学矫正（CNN-LSTM-RL），无需额外神经数据即可显著提升三维运动想象BCI的连续解码精度，在2D和VR场景下相关系数分别提升41.5%和21.2%，RMSE分别降低40.2%和38.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于非侵入式EEG的BCI在解码连续三维运动想象时存在信号变异性大、残差解码误差持续存在的问题，现有CNN-LSTM等深度学习模型虽能捕捉时空动态特征，但预测轨迹中仍有系统性残差误差。

Method: 提出两阶段解码框架：第一阶段使用CNN-LSTM模型进行初始运动学解码；第二阶段训练一个离线RL智能体，不直接输入EEG信号，而是在预测的运动学轨迹上操作，以目标轨迹为优化目标矫正残差误差。

Result: 与单独使用CNN-LSTM相比，CNN-LSTM-RL在2D场景下平均相关系数从0.5076提升至0.7181（p=0.0005，增益41.5%），RMSE从0.0890降至0.0532（p<0.0001，降低40.2%）；VR场景下相关系数从0.6420提升至0.7780（p=0.0059，增益21.2%），RMSE从0.0714降至0.0441（p<0.0001，降低38.2%）。

Conclusion: 该可扩展框架通过离线残差RL矫正运动学误差，无需额外神经数据即可增强三维BCI运动想象解码，对神经康复、假肢控制和虚拟交互具有推进意义。

Abstract: Decoding continuous three-dimensional (3D) motor imagery (MI) using non-invasive electroencephalography (EEG)-based brain--computer interfaces (BCIs) remains challenging due to signal variability and residual decoding errors. Deep learning architectures such as convolutional neural network--long short-term memory (CNN--LSTM) models can capture spatial and temporal dynamics for continuous kinematic decoding; however, systematic residual errors persist in predicted trajectories. We propose a two-stage decoding framework that applies reinforcement learning (RL) to perform residual kinematic correction on the outputs of a CNN--LSTM decoder (CNN--LSTM--RL). The RL agent is trained offline without direct EEG input and instead operates on predicted kinematic trajectories to optimize movement accuracy relative to target trajectories. Decoding performance was quantified using Pearson correlation coefficients ($r$) and Root Mean Square Errors (RMSE) along the $x, y$, and $z$ axes. Compared to CNN--LSTM applied alone, CNN--LSTM--RL improved the mean correlation from $0.5076$ to $0.7181$ ($p = 0.0005$) in 2D and from $0.6420$ to $0.7780$ ($p = 0.0059$) in VR, with relative gains of $41.5\%$ and $21.2\%$, respectively. Correspondingly, RMSE was reduced from $0.0890$ to $0.0532$ (2D, $p < 0.0001$) and from $0.0714$ to $0.0441$ (VR, $p < 0.0001$), representing relative reductions of $40.2\%$ and $38.2\%$. These findings demonstrate that this scalable framework enhances 3D BCI MI decoding by correcting kinematic errors via offline residual RL without extra neural data, advancing neurorehabilitation, prosthetics, and virtual interaction.

</details>


### [390] [HCRMap: Pressure-Aware Hot-Expert Residency Mapping for 3.5D MoE Chiplet Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11586)
*Yongqin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对3.5D多芯片系统中MoE大模型推理的专家热点倾斜问题，提出HCRMap框架，通过热专家副本的动态放置与重用在不同内存层级间联合缓解通信、内存和队列瓶颈，在预填充和解码阶段分别降低端到端延迟43.6%和43.0%（vs Hydra）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE LLM在推理时仅激活少量专家，但token路由导致持续的专家热点倾斜：少数热专家持续接收大部分token，其他专家负载极轻。在3.5D多芯片系统中，这种倾斜不仅造成计算不均衡，还加剧了通信、内存带宽、I/O和执行队列的压力。核心问题并非简单减少token移动，而是需要在不同内存层级间动态放置和重用热专家副本。

Method: 提出HCRMap（热专家驻留映射框架），基于专家热度、权重加载成本、迁移开销和运行时资源压力，动态决定哪些专家应被提升、保留、降级或驱逐，然后将路由的token组映射到合适的驻留副本，从而联合缓解通信、内存和队列瓶颈。

Result: 与Hydra相比，预填充阶段延迟降低43.6%，解码阶段降低43.0%；与MoEntwine相比分别降低34.5%和33.1%；与PIMoE相比分别降低46.7%和46.0%。

Conclusion: HCRMap通过压力感知的专家副本管理，有效解决了3.5D多芯片系统中MoE推理的专家热点倾斜问题，显著降低了端到端推理延迟。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models (LLM) activate only a small number of experts during inference, but token routing introduces persistent expert hotness skew: a small set of hot experts continuously receives most tokens, while the remaining experts are lightly loaded. On 3.5D multi-chiplet systems, this skew not only causes compute imbalance but also amplifies pressure on communication, memory bandwidth, I/O, and execution queues. Therefore, the core problem is not simply to reduce token movement, but to dynamically place and reuse hot expert replicas across different memory tiers.
  This paper proposes HCRMap, a hot expert residency mapping framework for pressure-aware expert replica management in 3.5D MoE inference. Based on expert hotness, weight loading cost, migration overhead, and runtime resource pressure, HCRMap dynamically determines which experts should be promoted, retained, demoted, or evicted. It then maps routed token groups to suitable resident replicas, thereby jointly mitigating communication, memory, and queue bottlenecks.
  Experimental results show that HCRMap reduces end-to-end latency by 43.6% and 43.0% over Hydra in the prefill and decode stages, respectively; by 34.5% and 33.1% over MoEntwine; and by 46.7% and 46.0% over PIMoE.

</details>


### [391] [Interaction Scaling: Grounding the Third Axis of Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11598)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出第三种测试时计算扩展方式——交互（Interaction），即模型生成工件、外部仪器观察实际行为、模型据此修正的循环。与推理扩展（内部推理更长）和采样扩展（多次采样选取最佳）不同，交互通过每次循环引入真实观测突破了前两者的性能天花板。关键在于"接地性"（grounding）：反馈和评估必须来自实际观测缺陷的仪器。在编码任务中，交互策略持续改进并达到100%通过率；在视觉工件任务中，基于截图评判会隐藏缺陷，而真实测量工具（接地）能有效修复40-74%的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算扩展方式（推理扩展和采样扩展）存在根本性局限：所有额外token都来自相同的冻结权重和相同提示，无法引入模型不知道的新信息。作者希望探索第三种方式——交互，通过引入外部真实观测来突破这一天花板。

Method: 提出"交互"范式：模型生成工件→外部仪器观察实际行为→模型修订，构成循环。关键在于"接地性"——反馈信号和评估指标都必须来自实际观测缺陷的仪器，而非模型自身的内部表征。在编码任务中采用proposer-reviewer框架，在视觉任务中使用真实布局测量工具作为接地反馈。

Result: 1）固定token预算下，推理扩展和最佳N采样均出现平台期，而交互策略持续改进；2）proposer-reviewer架构在编码任务中达到100%通过率且零方差；3）使用VLM截图评判会错误地将14/15个明显缺陷评为"完美"，而接地工具能检测并修复40-74%缺陷；4）非接地的VLM评审者会使幻灯片布局更差，而接地测量工具则修复问题。

Conclusion: 交互缩放（Interaction scaling）是一种真实存在且区别于推理和采样的测试时计算扩展方式，但只有当反馈和评估指标都具备接地性时才有效。非接地的评判者会隐藏缺陷，导致交互失效甚至恶化性能。

Abstract: There are two standard ways to spend more compute at test time: let a model reason longer, or sample more attempts and keep one. Both share a hidden limit: they are internal. Every extra token comes from the same frozen weights and the same prompt, so neither can tell the model anything it does not already know. We study a third way, interaction: the model proposes an artifact, an external instrument observes how it actually behaves, and the model revises. Each cycle imports a real observation, so interaction breaks through the ceiling the other two hit.
  We argue that a single variable governs this third axis, grounding, and that it must hold on both sides of the loop. The feedback that drives revision must come from an instrument that actually observes the flaw, and so must the metric that scores the result. On hard coding tasks at a fixed token budget, reasoning-only and best-of-N sampling both plateau (the latter even when an oracle picks the best sample), while every interaction strategy keeps improving; our proposer-reviewer harness reaches a perfect 100% pass rate with no run-to-run variance, and the gain holds across three model families. On rendered visual artifacts, the usual judge (a vision-language model, or VLM, reading a screenshot) rates 14 of 15 visibly broken figures "perfect," because the screenshot hides the flaws before the judge can see them. A tool that measures the real layout instead shows the loop removing 40-74% of defects across four modalities; and that same VLM, used as the reviewer, makes slide layouts worse where the measuring tool repairs them. Interaction scaling is real and distinct from reasoning and sampling, but only visible when both the feedback and the metric are grounded.

</details>


### [392] [Auditing the Risk Claims of Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11607)
*Hari Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统的统计审计方法，发现分布强化学习智能体（QR-DQN、C51、IQN）声称的风险偏好（如CVaR）在40-95%的情况下是虚假的，反映的是训练伪影而非环境随机性，且即使在Atari全尺度上也无法确认其风险声明的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习智能体学习完整的回报分布，并越来越多地被用于风险敏感控制、可解释性和安全监控。然而，理论预期这些学习到的分布应反映真实环境随机性，但这一假设从未被直接测量验证。作者想知道：训练后的分布智能体的风险声称是否真实可信？

Method: 提出一套审计框架，包括：(1) 决策相关的筛选指标——前两个动作之间的Wasserstein差距（等于一阶随机占优被违反的质量）；(2) 使用snapshot-restart蒙特卡洛方法获取真实风险基准；(3) 使用置换检验零假设、bootstrap反驳、FDR控制的统计保障机制。在MinAtar上对QR-DQN、C51、IQN进行33次运行测试，并在Atari全尺度上进行验证。同时设置阳性对照验证审计工具本身的有效性。

Result: (1) MinAtar上40-95%的最强声称风险权衡在95%置信水平下被驳斥；(2) 最强声明的放置位置与随机猜测无统计差异；(3) 几乎没有任何声明可以被确认；(4) 伪影是结构性的：在训练早期即完全形成，与最终得分不相关，对每个种子是特异的；(5) 在Atari全尺度上，预训练QR-DQN在Breakout上的所有顶级声明均被驳斥；(6) 阳性对照确认了96-100%的真实声明（相关性0.89-0.92）；(7) 基于CVaR建议在最高标记状态上行动，效果从有益到显著差于随机不等；(8) 风险训练或集成无法去除伪影，重新校准只有通过废除声明才能通过审计。

Conclusion: 分布强化学习智能体学到的风险分布反映的是训练伪影而非环境真实随机性，这些风险声称不可信赖。这表明当前分布强化学习的风险评估方法存在根本性问题，需要重新审视其在实际风险敏感应用中的可靠性。作者公开了审计工具包并记录了两种产生误导性审计结果的隐蔽陷阱。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning agents learn full return distributions that are increasingly read at face value: for interpretability, risk-sensitive control, and safety monitoring. We ask a question theory anticipates but that has not been measured directly: are the risk claims of a trained distributional agent true? Our audit combines a decision-relevant screening metric (the excess Wasserstein gap between the top two actions, which equals the mass by which first-order stochastic dominance is violated), ground truth from snapshot-restart Monte Carlo, and a statistical harness (permutation nulls, bootstrap refutation, FDR control) without which the audit itself manufactures false conclusions. Across QR-DQN, C51, and IQN on MinAtar (33 runs), 40-95% of the strongest claimed risk trade-offs are refuted at 95% confidence, the placement of the strongest claims is statistically indistinguishable from truth-blind, and essentially no claim is confirmable: for these agents, the learned "risk" reflects a training artifact rather than environment stochasticity. The artifact is structural (fully formed early in training, uncorrelated with final score, idiosyncratic to each seed) and appears unchanged at full-Atari scale, with every top Breakout claim of a pretrained near-state-of-the-art QR-DQN refuted. Positive controls of known magnitude confirm 96-100% of real claims (correlation 0.89-0.92): the reading measures the agents, not the audit. Acting on the heads' CVaR advice at their most-flagged states ranges from beneficial to significantly worse than chance. Neither training for risk nor ensembling removes the artifact, and recalibration passes the audit only by nullifying the claims: the head is uninformative, not merely miscalibrated. We release the toolkit and document two silent pitfalls that produced convincing but wrong audits of our own.

</details>


### [393] [Lesioned Multimodal Language Models Reproduce Aphasic Picture-Naming Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11621)
*Yong Yang,Xiang Guan,Sophie Arheix-Parras,Saeed Ahmadi,Roger Newman-Norlund,Leonardo Bonilha,Christopher Rorden,Julius Fridriksson,Rutvik H. Desai,Srihari Nelakuditi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过向多模态语言模型LLaVA 1.6施加受控扰动，该研究成功再现了278名失语症患者在图片命名任务中的七类错误模式，其中97.8%的患者在至少六个类别上得到匹配，79.5%的患者完全匹配。蒙特卡洛基线验证表明匹配反映的是联合类别间结构而非边缘重叠。该框架为失语症个体错误模式建立了定量复现方法，提示语言模型有潜力成为卒中后失语症患者的数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卒中后失语症会产生系统性的命名错误模式，但通用语言模型（非专为临床模拟设计）能否复现这些模式尚未得到验证。

Method: 使用LLaVA 1.6多模态语言模型，系统改变扰动配置（层、比例、噪声量），对278名失语症患者在费城命名测试中的反应进行分类（使用经过验证的神经分类器将反应分为七类），并搜索扰动空间以匹配个体错误轮廓。

Result: 六类反应（正确、语义、混合、无关、新词、无反应）在特定参数空间区域以临床可比比例出现；97.8%的患者在至少六个类别上匹配，79.5%的患者在全部七个类别上匹配；蒙特卡洛基线确认匹配反映了类别间联合结构。

Conclusion: 建立了一个定量框架用于复现失语症个体在图片命名中的错误模式，表明语言模型有潜力作为卒中后失语症个体的数字孪生。

Abstract: Aphasia following stroke commonly produces systematic naming errors with characteristic profiles, but whether general-purpose language models not designed for clinical simulation can reproduce these patterns remains untested. We investigated (1) whether lesions or controlled perturbations to a multimodal language model can reproduce different types of errors in picture naming, and (2) whether the framework can reproduce the complete error profile of individual persons with aphasia (PWAs). Using LLaVA 1.6, we evaluated perturbation configurations that varied the layer, proportion, and amount of noise applied to model units. We examined 278 PWAs on the Philadelphia Naming Test, classifying responses into seven categories using a validated neural classifier. Six of seven response categories (correct, semantic, mixed, unrelated, neologism, no response errors) emerged at clinically-comparable proportions across distinct parameter space regions, with formal paraphasia being the exception. Searching the perturbation space revealed configurations that reproduced the individual error profile in at least six of seven categories for 97.8% of PWAs and in all seven categories for 79.5% of PWAs. Monte Carlo baselines confirmed that this matching reflects joint inter-category structure rather than marginal overlap. These results establish a quantitative framework for reproducing individual aphasic error patterns in picture naming. They suggest the potential for language models to serve as digital twins of individuals with post-stroke aphasia.

</details>


### [394] [Reproducing human biases in route choice using large language models: Toward scalable behavioral modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11632)
*Jiangtao Han,Shoufeng Ma,Shuxian Xu,Geng Li,Shuai Ling,Ning Jia,Zhengbing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型（LLM）能否在没有明确设定前景理论参数的情况下，复现人类在路线选择等决策中的非理性行为偏差。实验表明，LLM能够生成与累积前景理论（CPT）预测一致的行为模式，为大规模智能体建模和AI驱动的行为研究提供了可扩展的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 累积前景理论（CPT）虽然能有效刻画人类决策中的系统性行为偏差，但大规模应用（如仿真和基于智能体的建模）需要指定个体层面的CPT参数，而传统依赖调查和受控实验的标定方法难以推广，且无法捕捉人类决策的多样性。

Method: 以路线选择为代表性场景，设计行为评估框架，系统比较LLM生成的决策与CPT预测的已有人类行为模式。

Result: LLM能够复现非理性的人类选择偏差，并在不确定性下表现出与前景理论效应一致的决策行为。

Conclusion: 生成式AI模型可能为建模人类决策过程提供可扩展的替代方案，并为下一代大规模基于智能体的仿真和AI驱动的行为研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Human choice behavior, including route choice, exhibits systematic behavioral biases that deviate from the assumptions of full rationality. Cumulative prospect theory (CPT) has been widely recognized as an effective framework for characterizing such behavioral patterns. However, its large-scale application, particularly in simulation and agent-based modeling, critically depends on specifying individual-level CPT parameters, which remain a major bottleneck. Conventional approaches typically rely on surveys and controlled experiments to calibrate CPT parameters, yet these methods are difficult to generalize and often fail to capture the full diversity of human decision-making. To address this challenge, this paper investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can reproduce human behavioral biases in choice-making without explicit specification of prospect-theoretic parameters. Using route choice as a representative scenario, we design a behavioral evaluation framework and systematically compare LLM-generated decisions with established human behavioral patterns predicted by CPT. Experimental results demonstrate that LLMs are capable of reproducing non-rational human choice biases and can exhibit decision behaviors consistent with prospect-theoretic effects under uncertainty. These findings suggest that generative AI models may provide a scalable alternative for modeling human decision processes and offer a promising foundation for next-generation large-scale agent-based simulation and AI-driven behavioral research.

</details>


### [395] [Think Through a Bottleneck: Hourglass Reasoning for Rigorous Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11696)
*Huan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出沙漏推理(Hourglass reasoning)方法，通过在推理阶段间实施严格上下文隔离，仅允许压缩符号状态(规则φ和变换T)跨阶段传递，显著提升了冻结大语言模型的少样本归纳推理能力。在ARC-AGI-2、ChipBench和BBEH-Linguini三个基准上，该方法均显著优于迭代细化基线，消融实验证明收益来源于阶段隔离而非提示措辞或符号形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，让LLM显式声明其推断规则或进行自我精炼(self-refinement)对提升少样本归纳推理能力效果有限。关键在于推理阶段之间需要结构性强制的隔离，使得信息只能以压缩的符号状态进行传递，而非自由流动的文本。

Method: 沙漏推理包含五个模块：(1) Induction模块将支持示例压缩为模式φ(编码器)和临时支架z；(2) Deduction模块从φ和z推导规则T(解码器)并丢弃z；(3) Implementer将(φ, T)编译为工件；(4) Refiner基于错误驱动修正(φ, T)并从头重新生成工件。整个过程中仅有(φ, T)跨阶段边界传递，所有精炼都锚定在规则上。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-2上，5次最佳准确率比迭代细化基线提升最高14个百分点；在ChipBench上，使用GPT-5.5时Verilog综合准确率从31%提升至58%；在BBEH-Linguini上，缓解了显式口头化损害性能的倾向，在Gemini 3.1 Pro上完全逆转了该效应。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实性能提升来源于阶段间的隔离和初始归纳的质量，而非提示措辞或特定符号形式。信息在推理过程中的流动方式（而非表达所用的语言）是驱动冻结LLM归纳推理的关键因素。

Abstract: Self-refinement often fails to strengthen few-shot inductive reasoning in large language models. Prompting a model to explicitly state its inferred rule does little on its own. What actually matters is a structurally enforced isolation between reasoning stages, so that information can only pass between them as a compressed symbolic state.
  We introduce \textbf{Hourglass reasoning}, which enforces strict context isolation between reasoning stages. The frozen LLM acts as a meta-constructor, building for each task a symbolic encoder--decoder: an Induction module compresses the support examples into a schema $φ$ (encoder) and a transient scaffold $z$; a Deduction module derives rule $T$ (decoder) from these and discards $z$; an Implementer compiles $(φ, T)$ into artifacts; an error-driven Refiner revises $(φ, T)$ and regenerates artifacts from scratch. Only $(φ, T)$ crosses stage boundaries, so all refinement stays anchored to the rule.
  We evaluate Hourglass across three benchmarks spanning visual abstraction, hardware synthesis, and textual rule induction, using GPT-5.5 and Gemini 3.1 Pro. On ARC-AGI-2, it raises best-of-5 accuracy by up to 14 points over an iterative-refinement baseline. On ChipBench, it nearly doubles Verilog synthesis accuracy with GPT-5.5, from 31\% to 58\%. BBEH-Linguini draws on puzzles from the International Linguistics Olympiad, a setting where prior work has shown that explicit verbalization can hurt performance. Hourglass mitigates this tendency, and on Gemini 3.1 Pro, it reverses the effect entirely.
  Ablations confirm that these gains come from the isolation between stages and the quality of the initial induction, not from prompt wording or the particular symbolic form used. It is how information flows through the reasoning process, rather than the language used to express it, that drives inductive reasoning in frozen LLMs.

</details>


### [396] [Playful AI in Professional Email: A Field Experiment on Tone and Recipient Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11749)
*Ziv Ben-Zion,Teddy Lazebnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过随机交叉田野实验（121名员工，16,880封邮件），发现GPT-5改写邮件（趣味/专业语气）虽显著改变情感基调，但并未直接影响打开率、回复率或响应时间；相反，发件人自身的积极情感通过间接路径显著预测收件人行为（打开OR=2.05，回复OR=3.32），表明AI辅助沟通通过改变语言情感基调间接影响工作参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI辅助写作是否改变收件人的实际行为及其作用渠道，填补AI沟通影响实证研究的空白。

Method: 随机交叉田野实验：121名员工在3周内分别在三种条件下（无辅助、GPT-5趣味改写、GPT-5专业改写）发送工作邮件，共分析16,880封邮件的行为数据与情感分析。

Result: 趣味编辑提升情感积极性（B=+0.068, p<0.001），专业编辑降低情感积极性（B=-0.041, p<0.001）；但两种编辑均未直接影响打开率、回复率或响应时间；发件人情感积极性显著预测打开（OR=2.05）和回复（OR=3.32, p<0.001），构成显著的间接效应路径。

Conclusion: AI辅助沟通不是通过AI的使用本身，而是通过其产出的语言情感基调来间接影响工作场所的参与行为。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly reshaping workplace communication, yet whether AI-assisted writing changes how recipients actually behave, and through what channel, remains unknown. Here, in a randomized crossover field experiment, 121 employees across six companies sent work emails under three conditions over three weeks: unaided writing, GPT-5 rewriting in a playful tone, and GPT-5 rewriting in a professional tone. Across 16,880 emails, playful editing increased emotional positivity (B=+0.068, p<0.001), and professional editing decreased it (B=-0.041, p<0.001), yet neither condition directly altered open rates, reply rates, or response times. Instead, within-sender positivity strongly predicted both opening (OR=2.05) and replying (OR=3.32, p<0.001), a significant indirect pathway through which AI editing shaped behavior, in the absence of any direct effect. These findings suggest that AI-assisted communication shapes workplace engagement not through its use, but through the emotional tone of the language it produces.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [397] [Learn2Chat: Rethinking Dyadic Talking Heads via Interaction-Modulated Monologic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10313)
*Zikai Huang,Siyue Chen,Xuemiao Xu,Haoxin Yang,Cheng Xu,Yihong Lin,Shengfeng He*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Learn2Chat提出了一种统一框架，通过将双人对话运动分解为预训练单人运动先验上的交互调制，实现了更高效、更解耦的双人对话运动生成，在DualTalk基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双人对话运动生成方法通常采用统一生成器建模所有行为，导致自我语音驱动运动与社交反馈耦合，未能充分利用预训练单人运动模型中已学习的语音-运动对应关系，交互特定成分未被充分挖掘。

Method: 提出单人对齐运动分解方案，利用从单数据学习的语义运动流形分离出音频驱动运动与交互调制，得到纯净交互表示；再通过跨分支注意力与交互对齐的交互潜在预测模块，将成对语音信号映射为交互潜在变量，在推理时调制标准单人运动生成双人行为。

Result: 在DualTalk基准上，定量指标与感知评估均达到最先进水平；框架与多种预训练单人运动骨干兼容，验证了先验复用与交互适应的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将双人交互建模为对单人运动先验的调制，实现了更结构化的交互建模与数据高效的生成，且模型无关的设计便于扩展。

Abstract: Dyadic conversational motion generation is essential for realistic interactive digital humans. Existing approaches typically model conversational behaviors within unified dyadic generators. However, such holistic formulations tend to couple self-speech-driven motion with partner-responsive social feedback, leaving the interaction-specific component implicit and underutilizing the speech-motion correspondence already learned by pretrained monologic motion models. We propose Learn2Chat, a unified framework that models dyadic motion as interaction modulation over pretrained monologic motion priors. This design separates intrinsic speech-driven motion from social interaction effects and enables more structured interaction modeling. Specifically, we introduce a Monologic-Anchored Motion Factorization scheme that leverages the semantic motion manifold learned from monologic data to disentangle audio-driven motion dynamics from interaction-induced modulation, yielding clean interaction representations from dyadic sequences. On top of this representation space, a Cross-Attentive Interaction Latent Prediction module maps paired speech signals to interaction latents through cross-branch attention and interaction alignment. During inference, the predicted interaction latents modulate canonical monologic motion to generate coherent and synchronized dyadic behaviors in a data-efficient manner. Extensive experiments on the DualTalk benchmark demonstrate that Learn2Chat achieves state-of-the-art performance across both quantitative metrics and perceptual evaluations. Moreover, the framework is model-agnostic and seamlessly integrates with diverse pretrained monologic motion backbones, highlighting the effectiveness of prior reuse and interaction adaptation for scalable conversational motion generation. More visual results are available on the project page.

</details>


### [398] [PoseAlign: Sculpting Pose-Consistent Meshes via Text-Guided Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10560)
*Shijin Wang,Zichong Chen,Yang Zhou,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: PoseAlign提出了一种两阶段文本驱动网格变形方法：全局姿态缩放（基于可微拉普拉斯表示）和局部细节雕刻（基于注意力共享的SDS损失），在保持原始姿态的同时实现与文本提示对齐的网格变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动的3D网格变形方法难以同时满足文本提示的语义要求并保持原始网格姿态，两者之间存在矛盾。

Method: 1) 第一阶段：将拉普拉斯算子作为可微网格表示，实现更高效平滑的全局变形；2) 第二阶段：提出姿态对齐的SDS损失，引入注意力共享机制，在雕刻细粒度几何细节的同时保持原始姿态。

Result: 在文本对齐和网格质量方面相比现有方法具有竞争优势。

Conclusion: PoseAlign显著增强了变形过程的可控性，在姿态保持与文本对齐之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Mesh deformation, the process of altering the vertex positions of a 3D mesh while preserving its topological structure, is a cornerstone of computer graphics. Despite the recent emergence of numerous text-guided 3D mesh deformation methods, deforming an initial mesh into one that both adheres to text prompts and preserves its pose remains challenging. This paper proposes PoseAlign, which decomposes text-guided mesh deformation into two stages: global pose scaling and local detail sculpting. Specifically, in the first stage, we introduce the Laplacian as a differentiable mesh representation to enable more efficient yet smoother global deformation. Then, we propose a novel pose-aligned SDS loss by adapting score distillation sampling (SDS) with an attention-sharing mechanism, which sculptures fine-grained geometric details for the deformed mesh while preserving its original pose. PoseAlign significantly enhances the controllability of the overall deformation process, achieving a favorable balance between pose preservation and text alignment. Experiments demonstrate the competitive advantages of our method in text alignment and mesh quality. Code is available at: https://cousingrade6.github.io/PoseAlign

</details>


### [399] [LATO.2: Factorized 3D Mesh Generation with Vertex and Topology Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10623)
*Hang Long,Tianhao Zhao,Junkai Lin,Youjia Zhang,Huipeng Guo,Rendong Liang,Jiale Xu,Jozef Hladký,Matthias Nießner,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: LATO.2提出了一种分解式流匹配框架，将网格生成解耦为顶点流和基于顶点的连接流，两阶段均锚定在共享粗体素支架上，实现了几何保真度和连接质量的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在联合隐空间中建模顶点几何与离散连接结构，导致顶点漂移和表面破裂。LATO.2旨在通过分解生成过程解决这一耦合问题。

Method: 提出因子化流匹配框架，分解为顶点流和条件连接流；两阶段分别使用专用VAE，在子体素精度恢复顶点并将离散连接嵌入连续隐空间；共享粗体素支架作为锚定。

Result: 在几何保真度和连接质量上超越当前最先进的拓扑感知网格生成方法；实现了部件级高分辨率生成和拓扑自适应编辑。

Conclusion: 分解式生成范式能有效解耦几何与拓扑，带来更高分辨率生成能力和灵活编辑能力。

Abstract: Flow matching over carefully designed latent representations has recently emerged as a powerful paradigm for topology-aware mesh generation. Existing approaches, however, model vertices and connectivity jointly in a joint latent space, entangling continuous vertex geometry with discrete combinatorial structure; this complicates flow learning and manifests as drifting vertices and broken surfaces. We present LATO.2, a factorized flow matching framework that decomposes mesh generation into a vertex flow followed by a connectivity flow conditioned on the realized vertices, with both stages anchored to a shared coarse voxel scaffold. Dedicated VAEs underpin the two stages, recovering vertices at sub-voxel precision and embedding discrete connectivity into a continuous latent space. We demonstrate two advantages unique to this factorization: (i) part-wise generation, in which the scaffold is partitioned and each part synthesized at full latent capacity, yielding substantially higher-resolution meshes than a monolithic latent permits; and (ii) topology-adaptive editing, in which manipulating first-stage vertices induces the corresponding connectivity without re-optimization. Experiments show that LATO.2 surpasses state-of-the-art topology-aware mesh generators in geometric fidelity and connectivity quality.

</details>


### [400] [IBPA: Real-time Free-form Manifold Mesh Reconstruction via Incremental Ball Pivoting with Integrated Hole Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11627)
*Mauhing Yip,Mohit Singh,Kostas Alexis,Christian Schellewald,Annette Stahl*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出增量式球旋转算法（IBPA），用于水下机器人实时三维表面重建，支持复杂拓扑结构并检测数据空洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下勘测成本高昂，传统DTM方法无法表达悬垂或垂直结构，且现有方法需要先验假设，无法实时增量重建。

Method: 将球旋转算法（BPA）改造为增量式、实时的自由曲面重建方法，从流式点云数据逐步构建可定向流形网格，并引入空洞检测机制。

Result: 支持更复杂的曲面拓扑，无需点云重叠或空间分布假设，可实时可视化表面覆盖范围并标识不完整区域。

Conclusion: IBPA有效克服了传统DTM的局限性，为水下作业提供实时、自由曲面的表面重建能力。

Abstract: Both Remotely Operated underwater Vehicles (ROVs) and Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) are frequently deployed to acquire geometric bathymetric data. However, it is often discovered post-survey that the acquired data coverage is incomplete. Given the high operational cost associated with underwater deployments, it is essential to incrementally visualize surface coverage in real-time to support informed decision-making by both the operators of ROVs and the AUVs during data collection. In addition, traditional incremental surface reconstruction methods, such as Digital Terrain Models (DTMs), are inherently limited in expressiveness: they represent surfaces as height fields, allows only one elevation value per $(x, y)$ coordinate and thus cannot capture overhangs or vertical structures.
  To overcome these limitations, we adapt the original Ball Pivoting Algorithm (BPA) into an incremental, real-time, and free-form surface reconstruction method, referred to as Incremental BPA (IBPA). Our method incrementally constructs an orientable, manifold mesh from streaming point cloud data without imposing assumptions regarding point cloud overlap or spatial distribution. Furthermore, we introduce a hole detection mechanism that identifies and highlights incomplete mesh regions. Compared to existing approaches, our method supports more complex surface topologies without prior structural assumptions.
  The source code of our reference implementation is available: https://github.com/Mauhing/Incremental-BPA

</details>
